// ==============================================================================
// FILE: src/app/api/checkout/create-order/route.ts
// ==============================================================================

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import crypto from "crypto";
import { getProductByIdAdmin, createOrder } from "@/lib/store";
import { calculateShippingFee, DEFAULT_PRICING_SETTINGS, calculateContribution } from "@/lib/pricing-engine";
import { OrderItem, CustomerAddress } from "@/lib/types";

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { customer, items } = body as {
      customer: CustomerAddress;
      items: Array<{ productId: string; variantId?: string; quantity: number }>;
    };

    if (!customer || !customer.name || !customer.email || !customer.phone || !customer.pincode) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Missing required customer address fields." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    if (customer.country !== "India") {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "International checkout is currently unavailable. We currently deliver within India only." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!items || !Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Your shopping bag is empty." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // Verify each item against server store
    const verifiedItems: OrderItem[] = [];
    let serverSubtotal = 0;
    let totalSupplierCost = 0;

    for (const item of items) {
      const product = getProductByIdAdmin(item.productId);
      if (!product || !product.is_visible) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          { error: `A product in your bag is no longer available.` },
          { status: 400 }
        );
      }

      const variant = item.variantId ? product.variants.find((v) => v.id === item.variantId) : undefined;
      const unitSellingPrice = variant?.price_override ?? product.selling_price;
      const unitSupplierCost = product.supplier_cost;
      const qty = Math.max(1, Number(item.quantity) || 1);

      // Check stock
      const availableStock = variant ? variant.stock : product.stock;
      if (qty > availableStock) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          { error: `Insufficient stock for ${product.name}. Available: ${availableStock}` },
          { status: 400 }
        );
      }

      const lineTotal = unitSellingPrice * qty;
      const lineCost = unitSupplierCost * qty;

      serverSubtotal += lineTotal;
      totalSupplierCost += lineCost;

      verifiedItems.push({
        id: `ord-item-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
        product_id: product.id,
        variant_id: variant?.id,
        product_name: product.name,
        variant_title: variant?.title,
        sku: variant?.sku || product.sku,
        unit_selling_price: unitSellingPrice,
        unit_supplier_cost: unitSupplierCost,
        quantity: qty,
        total_price: lineTotal,
        total_cost: lineCost,
        image_url: product.images[0]?.image_url,
      });
    }

    const shippingFee = calculateShippingFee(serverSubtotal, DEFAULT_PRICING_SETTINGS);
    const totalPayable = serverSubtotal + shippingFee;

    // Calculate contribution metrics
    const contribution = calculateContribution({
      sellingPrice: serverSubtotal,
      supplierCost: totalSupplierCost,
      shippingCustomerPaid: shippingFee,
    });

    // Save pending order to store
    const order = createOrder({
      customer,
      items: verifiedItems,
      status: "pending_payment",
      payment_status: "unpaid",
      subtotal: serverSubtotal,
      shipping_fee: shippingFee,
      discount_amount: 0,
      total_payable: totalPayable,
      total_supplier_cost: totalSupplierCost,
      total_contribution: contribution.netContribution,
    });

    // Check if Razorpay keys are configured in environment
    const razorpayKeyId = process.env.RAZORPAY_KEY_ID || process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_RAZORPAY_KEY_ID;
    const razorpaySecret = process.env.RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET;

    if (razorpayKeyId && razorpaySecret) {
      try {
        // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports
        const Razorpay = require("razorpay");
        const instance = new Razorpay({
          key_id: razorpayKeyId,
          key_secret: razorpaySecret,
        });

        const rzpOrder = await instance.orders.create({
          amount: Math.round(totalPayable * 100), // amount in paise
          currency: "INR",
          receipt: order.order_number,
          notes: {
            orderNumber: order.order_number,
            customerEmail: customer.email,
          },
        });

        return NextResponse.json({
          orderNumber: order.order_number,
          razorpayOrderId: rzpOrder.id,
          amountPaise: rzpOrder.amount,
          currency: "INR",
          keyId: razorpayKeyId,
          isLivePayment: true,
        });
      } catch (rzpErr) {
        console.error("Razorpay order creation failed, falling back to staging simulation:", rzpErr);
      }
    }

    // Safe simulation mode when live credentials are not yet configured
    const simulatedOrderId = `order_sim_${crypto.randomBytes(8).toString("hex")}`;
    return NextResponse.json({
      orderNumber: order.order_number,
      razorpayOrderId: simulatedOrderId,
      amountPaise: Math.round(totalPayable * 100),
      currency: "INR",
      keyId: "rzp_test_unconfigured",
      isLivePayment: false,
      message: "Gateway in demo/staging mode. Real credentials pending in .env.local.",
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Create order error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to create order. Please try again." }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


// ==============================================================================
// FILE: src/app/api/checkout/verify-payment/route.ts
// ==============================================================================

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import crypto from "crypto";
import { getOrderByNumber, updateOrderStatusAdmin } from "@/lib/store";

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { orderNumber, razorpayOrderId, razorpayPaymentId, razorpaySignature } = await req.json();

    if (!orderNumber || !razorpayOrderId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Missing verification parameters." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const order = getOrderByNumber(orderNumber);
    if (!order) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Order not found." }, { status: 404 });
    }

    const razorpaySecret = process.env.RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET;

    // If live credentials are provided, strictly verify HMAC SHA-256 signature
    if (razorpaySecret && razorpayPaymentId && razorpaySignature) {
      const generatedSignature = crypto
        .createHmac("sha256", razorpaySecret)
        .update(`${razorpayOrderId}|${razorpayPaymentId}`)
        .digest("hex");

      if (generatedSignature !== razorpaySignature) {
        console.error("Payment signature verification failed!");
        return NextResponse.json(
          { error: "Payment verification failed. Security signature mismatch." },
          { status: 400 }
        );
      }
    }

    // Update order status to Paid
    updateOrderStatusAdmin({
      orderNumber,
      status: "paid",
    });

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      orderNumber,
      status: "paid",
      message: "Payment successfully verified and order confirmed.",
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Payment verification error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Server payment verification failed." }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

// FILE: src/app/api/track-order/route.ts
// ==============================================================================

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { trackOrder, getOrderByNumber } from "@/lib/store";

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    const orderNumber = searchParams.get("orderNumber") || "";
    const contact = searchParams.get("contact") || "";

    if (!orderNumber) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Missing orderNumber" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const order = contact
      ? trackOrder(orderNumber, contact)
      : getOrderByNumber(orderNumber);

    if (!order) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Order not found" }, { status: 404 });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ order });
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Tracking error" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


// ==============================================================================
// FILE: src/app/api/webhooks/razorpay/route.ts
// ==============================================================================

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import crypto from "crypto";
import { updateOrderStatusAdmin, getOrderByNumber } from "@/lib/store";

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const rawBody = await req.text();
    const signature = req.headers.get("x-razorpay-signature");
    const webhookSecret = process.env.RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET;

    if (webhookSecret && signature) {
      const expectedSignature = crypto
        .createHmac("sha256", webhookSecret)
        .update(rawBody)
        .digest("hex");

      if (expectedSignature !== signature) {
        console.error("Invalid webhook signature received");
        return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid signature" }, { status: 400 });
      }
    }

    const payload = JSON.parse(rawBody);
    const event = payload.event;

    if (event === "payment.captured") {
      const paymentEntity = payload.payload?.payment?.entity;
      const orderReceipt = paymentEntity?.notes?.orderNumber;

      if (orderReceipt) {
        const order = getOrderByNumber(orderReceipt);
        if (order && order.status === "pending_payment") {
          updateOrderStatusAdmin({
            orderNumber: order.order_number,
            status: "paid",
          });
        }
      }
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ status: "ok" });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Webhook processing error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Webhook handler failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

  const [promoApplied, setPromoApplied] = useState(false);
  const [promoError, setPromoError] = useState("");

  const handleApplyPromo = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (promoCode.trim().toUpperCase() === "WELCOME10") {
      setPromoApplied(true);
      setPromoError("");

      setPromoError("Invalid code. Use WELCOME10 for 10% off your first order.");
    }
  };

  const discountAmount = promoApplied ? Math.round(subtotal * 0.1) : 0;
  const finalPayable = Math.max(0, totalPayable - discountAmount);

  if (items.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 py-24 text-center space-y-6">
        <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-ivory flex items-center justify-center mx-auto border border-champagne">
          <Sparkles className="w-8 h-8 text-taupe-dark" />
        </div>
        <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl sm:text-4xl text-charcoal">
          Your Shopping Bag is Empty
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70 font-sans max-w-md mx-auto font-light">
          Your keepsake collection awaits. Explore our delicate pearls, silk bows, and everyday boutique treasures.

          <Link href="/shop" className="btn-boutique-primary">
            Explore The Collection
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    );

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-12 sm:py-16 space-y-10">
      
      {/* Page Title */}
      <div className="border-b border-[#EBE3D8] pb-6">
        <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-taupe font-sans block mb-1">
          Review Your Selection
        </span>
        <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl sm:text-4xl text-charcoal">
          Shopping Bag ({items.reduce((s, i) => s + i.quantity, 0)})
        </h1>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-12 items-start">
        
        {/* Left Column: Cart Items List */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-8 space-y-6">
          
          {/* Free Shipping Alert Bar */}
          <div className="bg-ivory/60 border border-champagne/60 p-4">
            {amountNeededForFreeShipping > 0 ? (
              <p className="text-xs text-charcoal/80 font-sans">
                Add <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal">₹{amountNeededForFreeShipping}</span> more to unlock <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal">FREE Express Shipping</span> across India.
              </p>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-xs text-emerald-800 font-sans font-medium">
                ✨ Complimentary Boutique Express Shipping Unlocked!
              </p>
            )}
            <div className="w-full bg-[#E3D9CC] h-1.5 rounded-full mt-2 overflow-hidden">
              <div
                className="bg-taupe h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-500"
                style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, Math.round((subtotal / freeShippingThreshold) * 100))}%` }}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Items Table */}
          <div className="space-y-6">
            {items.map((item) => {
              const img = item.product.images[0]?.image_url || "";
              const unitPrice = item.variant?.price_override ?? item.product.selling_price;
              const lineTotal = unitPrice * item.quantity;

              return (
                <div
                  key={`${item.product.id}-${item.variant?.id || "default"}`}
                  className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-6 p-5 bg-white border border-[#EBE3D8] hover:border-champagne transition-all"

                  <div className="flex gap-4 items-center">
                    <div className="relative w-20 h-24 bg-ivory shrink-0 border border-[#EBE3D8] overflow-hidden">
                      {img && (
                        <Image
                          src={img}
                          alt={item.product.name}
                          fill
                          className="object-cover object-center"
                        />
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="space-y-1">
                      <span className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-taupe font-sans block">
                        {item.product.category.replace("-", " ")}
                      </span>
                      <Link
                        href={`/product/${item.product.slug}`}
                        className="font-serif text-lg text-charcoal hover:text-taupe transition-colors leading-snug line-clamp-1"
                      >
                        {item.product.name}
                      </Link>
                      {item.variant && (
                        <span className="text-xs text-taupe block font-sans">
                          Option: {item.variant.title}
                        </span>
                      )}
                      <span className="text-xs font-medium text-charcoal font-sans block sm:hidden">
                        ₹{unitPrice}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between sm:justify-end gap-6 border-t sm:border-t-0 pt-3 sm:pt-0 border-[#EBE3D8]">
                    {/* Quantity controls */}
                    <div className="flex items-center border border-champagne bg-white">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => updateQuantity(item.product.id, item.quantity - 1, item.variant?.id)}
                        className="p-2 text-charcoal hover:bg-ivory transition-colors"
                        aria-label="Decrease"
                      >
                        <Minus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      </button>
                      <span className="px-3 text-xs font-sans font-medium text-charcoal">
                        {item.quantity}
                      </span>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => updateQuantity(item.product.id, item.quantity + 1, item.variant?.id)}
                        className="p-2 text-charcoal hover:bg-ivory transition-colors"
                        aria-label="Increase"
                      >
                        <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    <div className="text-right min-w-[80px]">
                      <span className="font-sans text-sm font-medium text-charcoal">
                        ₹{lineTotal.toLocaleString("en-IN")}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => removeItem(item.product.id, item.variant?.id)}
                      className="text-taupe hover:text-rose-700 transition-colors p-1"
                      title="Remove"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          <div className="pt-2">
            <Link
              href="/shop"
              className="text-xs uppercase tracking-widest font-sans text-charcoal hover:text-taupe underline underline-offset-4"
            >
              &larr; Continue Shopping
            </Link>
          </div>

        </div>

        {/* Right Column: Order Summary */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-4 bg-white border border-[#EBE3D8] p-6 space-y-6">
          <h2 className="font-serif text-xl text-charcoal border-b border-[#EBE3D8] pb-4">
            Order Summary
          </h2>

          <div className="space-y-3 text-xs font-sans text-charcoal/80">
            <div className="flex justify-between">
              <span>Subtotal</span>
              <span>₹{subtotal.toLocaleString("en-IN")}</span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-between">
              <span>Estimated Shipping (India)</span>
              <span>{shippingFee === 0 ? "FREE" : `₹${shippingFee}`}</span>
            </div>

            {promoApplied && (
              <div className="flex justify-between text-emerald-800 font-medium">
                <span>Welcome Discount (10%)</span>
                <span>-₹{discountAmount.toLocaleString("en-IN")}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="border-t border-[#EBE3D8] pt-3 flex justify-between text-base font-medium text-charcoal">
              <span>Total Payable</span>
              <span>₹{finalPayable.toLocaleString("en-IN")}</span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-taupe text-right">Includes all taxes</p>
          </div>

          {/* Promo code form */}
          <form onSubmit={handleApplyPromo} className="border-t border-[#EBE3D8] pt-4 space-y-2">
            <label className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-sans text-charcoal block">
              Promotional Code
            </label>
            <div className="flex">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={promoCode}
                onChange={(e) => setPromoCode(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. WELCOME10"
                className="bg-ivory/50 border border-champagne px-3 py-2 text-xs text-charcoal focus:outline-none focus:border-charcoal flex-1 uppercase"
              />
              <button
                type="submit"
                className="bg-charcoal text-white text-xs uppercase tracking-wider px-4 py-2 font-sans hover:bg-taupe-dark transition-colors"
              >
                Apply
              </button>
            </div>
            {promoApplied && (
              <p className="text-xs text-emerald-700">✓ 10% Welcome discount applied!</p>
            )}
            {promoError && (
              <p className="text-xs text-rose-700">{promoError}</p>
            )}
          </form>

          {/* Proceed Button */}
          <div className="space-y-3 pt-2">
            <Link
              href="/checkout"
              className="btn-boutique-primary w-full py-4 text-center flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              <span>Proceed to Prepaid Checkout</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
            </Link>

            <div className="pt-3 border-t border-[#EBE3D8] space-y-2 text-[11px] text-taupe font-sans">
              <p className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-taupe-dark shrink-0" />
                Prepaid only &bull; Secured with 256-bit encryption
              </p>
              <p className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Truck className="w-4 h-4 text-taupe-dark shrink-0" />
                Dispatched in tamper-evident boutique box
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}


// ==============================================================================
// FILE: src/app/category/[slug]/page.tsx
// ==============================================================================

"use client";

import { use, useState, useMemo, useEffect } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import ProductCard from "@/components/ProductCard";
import { getPublicProducts } from "@/lib/public-catalog";
import { CATEGORIES_DATA } from "@/data/seed-data";
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowUpDown } from "lucide-react";

export default function CategoryPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
  const { slug } = use(params);

  const categoryMeta = CATEGORIES_DATA.find((c) => c.slug === slug);
  if (!categoryMeta) {
    notFound();
  }

  const [allProducts, setAllProducts] = useState(() => getPublicProducts({ category: slug }));

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch(`/api/products?category=${encodeURIComponent(slug)}`)
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (data?.products && Array.isArray(data.products) && data.products.length > 0) {
          setAllProducts(data.products);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, [slug]);

  const [selectedSub, setSelectedSub] = useState<string>("all");
  const [onlyHottest, setOnlyHottest] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState<string>("relevance");

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    let result = [...allProducts];
    if (onlyHottest) {
      result = result.filter((p) => p.is_bestseller);
    }
    if (selectedSub !== "all") {
      result = result.filter((p) => p.subcategory === selectedSub);
    }
    switch (sortBy) {
      case "price-asc":
        result.sort((a, b) => a.selling_price - b.selling_price);
        break;
      case "price-desc":
        result.sort((a, b) => b.selling_price - a.selling_price);
        break;
      case "hottest":
        result.sort((a, b) => (b.is_bestseller ? 1 : 0) - (a.is_bestseller ? 1 : 0));
        break;
      case "newest":
        result.sort((a, b) => (b.is_new ? 1 : 0) - (a.is_new ? 1 : 0));
        break;
      case "alpha":
        result.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
        break;
      case "relevance":
      default:
        result.sort((a, b) => {
          const scoreB = (b.is_bestseller ? 4 : 0) + (b.is_featured ? 2 : 0) + (b.is_new ? 1 : 0);
          const scoreA = (a.is_bestseller ? 4 : 0) + (a.is_featured ? 2 : 0) + (a.is_new ? 1 : 0);
          return scoreB - scoreA;
        });
        break;
    }
    return result;
  }, [allProducts, selectedSub, onlyHottest, sortBy]);

  const hottestCount = allProducts.filter((p) => p.is_bestseller).length;

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-12 sm:py-16 space-y-10">
      
      {/* Breadcrumb Navigation */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-sans text-taupe">
        <Link href="/" className="hover:text-charcoal transition-colors">Home</Link>
        <span>/</span>
        <Link href="/shop" className="hover:text-charcoal transition-colors">Collections</Link>
        <span>/</span>
        <span className="text-charcoal font-medium">{categoryMeta.name}</span>
      </div>

      {/* Editorial Category Header */}
      <div className="text-center max-w-2xl mx-auto space-y-4">
        <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-taupe font-sans block">
          Boutique Department
        </span>
        <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl sm:text-5xl text-charcoal">
          {categoryMeta.name}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-charcoal/70 font-sans font-light leading-relaxed">
          {categoryMeta.description}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Subcategory Pills & Controls */}
      <div className="border-y border-[#EBE3D8] py-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
        
        {/* Subcategories */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setSelectedSub("all");
              setOnlyHottest(false);
            }}
            className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-sans transition-all ${
              selectedSub === "all" && !onlyHottest
                ? "bg-charcoal text-white font-medium"
                : "bg-white border border-[#EBE3D8] text-charcoal/70 hover:border-champagne hover:text-charcoal"
            }`}
          >
            All {categoryMeta.name} ({allProducts.length})
          </button>

          {hottestCount > 0 && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setOnlyHottest(!onlyHottest)}
              className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-sans transition-all flex items-center gap-1 ${
                onlyHottest
                  ? "bg-[#2D2A26] text-[#FAF8F5] font-medium shadow-sm"
                  : "bg-[#FAF7F2] border border-champagne text-[#C2714F] hover:border-charcoal font-medium"
              }`}
            >
              <span>🔥 Hottest Picks</span>
              <span className="text-[10px] opacity-80">({hottestCount})</span>
            </button>
          )}

          {categoryMeta.subcategories.map((sub) => {
            const count = allProducts.filter((p) => p.subcategory === sub.id).length;
            const isSelected = selectedSub === sub.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={sub.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setSelectedSub(sub.id);
                  setOnlyHottest(false);
                }}
                className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-sans transition-all flex items-center gap-1 ${
                  isSelected
                    ? "bg-charcoal text-white font-medium"
                    : "bg-white border border-[#EBE3D8] text-charcoal/70 hover:border-champagne hover:text-charcoal"
                }`}
              >
                <span>{sub.name}</span>
                {count > 0 && (
                  <span className={`text-[10px] ${isSelected ? "opacity-80" : "text-taupe"}`}>
                    ({count})
                  </span>
                )}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Sort */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-sans text-charcoal/80">
          <ArrowUpDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-taupe" />
          <select
            value={sortBy}
            onChange={(e) => setSortBy(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-transparent text-charcoal text-xs uppercase tracking-wider focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
          >
            <option value="relevance">Relevance &bull; Curated</option>
            <option value="price-asc">Price: Low to High (₹)</option>
            <option value="price-desc">Price: High to Low (₹)</option>
            <option value="hottest">🔥 Hottest Picks First</option>
            <option value="newest">New Arrivals</option>
            <option value="alpha">Alphabetical (A - Z)</option>
          </select>
        </div>

      </div>

      {/* Product Grid */}
      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 bg-ivory/60 border border-champagne p-8 sm:p-12 space-y-4 max-w-xl mx-auto">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-white border border-champagne text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-charcoal font-sans">
            ✦ DROP 01 IN PREPARATION
          </div>
          <h3 className="font-serif text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-charcoal">
            {categoryMeta.name} Pieces Dropping Soon
          </h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-charcoal/70 font-sans leading-relaxed max-w-md mx-auto">
            Our atelier is stocking this collection with hand-selected pieces. Explore our other vibes or check back during the next drop.
          </p>
          <div className="pt-2">
            <Link href="/shop" className="btn-boutique-primary">
              View All Boutique Vibes
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-5 sm:gap-6">
          {filtered.map((product) => (
            <ProductCard key={product.id} product={product} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Back to shop */}
      <div className="pt-8 text-center">
        <Link href="/shop" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs uppercase tracking-widest font-sans text-charcoal hover:text-taupe transition-colors">
          <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>Explore All Categories</span>
        </Link>
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}


// ==============================================================================
// FILE: src/app/checkout/page.tsx
// ==============================================================================

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useCart } from "@/context/cart-context";
import { Lock, ShieldCheck, AlertCircle, ArrowLeft, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";

const INDIAN_STATES = [
  "Andhra Pradesh",
  "Arunachal Pradesh",
  "Assam",
  "Bihar",
  "Chhattisgarh",
  "Goa",
  "Gujarat",
  "Haryana",
  "Himachal Pradesh",
  "Jharkhand",
  "Karnataka",
  "Kerala",
  "Madhya Pradesh",
  "Maharashtra",
  "Manipur",
  "Meghalaya",
  "Mizoram",
  "Nagaland",
  "Odisha",
  "Punjab",
  "Rajasthan",
  "Sikkim",
  "Tamil Nadu",
  "Telangana",
  "Tripura",
  "Uttar Pradesh",
  "Uttarakhand",
  "West Bengal",
  "Delhi",
  "Chandigarh",
  "Jammu & Kashmir",
  "Ladakh",
  "Puducherry",
];

export default function CheckoutPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { items, subtotal, shippingFee, totalPayable, clearCart } = useCart();

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    name: "",
    email: "",
    phone: "",
    house: "",
    street: "",
    city: "",
    state: "Karnataka",
    pincode: "",
  });

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState("");
  const [demoNotice, setDemoNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (items.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto px-4 py-24 text-center space-y-4">
        <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl text-charcoal">Your bag is empty</h1>
        <p className="text-xs text-charcoal/70 font-sans">
          Please add items to your shopping bag before proceeding to checkout.
        </p>
        <Link href="/shop" className="btn-boutique-primary inline-block">
          Return to Shop
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const handleInputChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement>) => {
    const { name, value } = e.target;
    setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
  };

  const handleCheckoutSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setErrorMessage("");
    setDemoNotice(null);

    // Validate Indian PIN code (6 digits)
    if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(formData.pincode.trim())) {
      setErrorMessage("Please enter a valid 6-digit Indian PIN code.");
      return;
    }

    // Validate phone number (10 digits)
    const cleanPhone = formData.phone.replace(/\D/g, "");
    if (cleanPhone.length < 10) {
      setErrorMessage("Please enter a valid 10-digit mobile number.");
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);

    try {
      // 1. Create order on server
      const payload = {
        customer: {
          ...formData,
          phone: `+91 ${cleanPhone.slice(-10)}`,
          country: "India" as const,
        },
        items: items.map((i) => ({
          productId: i.product.id,
          variantId: i.variant?.id,
          quantity: i.quantity,
        })),
      };

      const res = await fetch("/api/checkout/create-order", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      });

      const orderData = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(orderData.error || "Failed to initialize checkout.");
      }

      const { orderNumber, razorpayOrderId, isLivePayment } = orderData;

      // 2. Check if Live Razorpay is active
      if (isLivePayment && typeof window !== "undefined") {
        // Load Razorpay Standard Modal script dynamically if not present
        if (!(window as unknown as { Razorpay?: unknown }).Razorpay) {
          await new Promise((resolve) => {
            const script = document.createElement("script");
            script.src = "https://checkout.razorpay.com/v1/checkout.js";
            script.onload = resolve;
            document.body.appendChild(script);
          });
        }

        interface RzpResponse {
          razorpay_order_id: string;
          razorpay_payment_id: string;
          razorpay_signature: string;
        }

        const options = {
          key: orderData.keyId,
          amount: orderData.amountPaise,
          currency: "INR",
          name: "Petal & Pearl",
          description: `Order ${orderNumber} - Prepaid Boutique Adornments`,
          order_id: razorpayOrderId,
          prefill: {
            name: formData.name,
            email: formData.email,
            contact: cleanPhone,
          },
          theme: {
            color: "#39342F",
          },
          handler: async function (response: RzpResponse) {
            // Server verification
            const verifyRes = await fetch("/api/checkout/verify-payment", {
              method: "POST",
              headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
              body: JSON.stringify({
                orderNumber,
                razorpayOrderId: response.razorpay_order_id,
                razorpayPaymentId: response.razorpay_payment_id,
                razorpaySignature: response.razorpay_signature,
              }),
            });

            const verifyData = await verifyRes.json();
            if (verifyRes.ok && verifyData.success) {
              clearCart();
              router.push(`/order-confirmation/${orderNumber}`);
            } else {
              setErrorMessage("Payment verification failed. Please contact boutique support.");
              setIsLoading(false);
            }
          },
          modal: {
            ondismiss: function () {
              setIsLoading(false);
            },
          },
        };

        // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
        const rzp = new (window as any).Razorpay(options);
        rzp.open();
        return;
      }

      // 3. Staging / Test fallback mode with clear UI indicator
      setDemoNotice("Live payment gateway keys not configured in .env.local. Simulating authorized test verification...");
      
      const verifyRes = await fetch("/api/checkout/verify-payment", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          orderNumber,
          razorpayOrderId,
          razorpayPaymentId: `pay_sim_${Date.now()}`,
          razorpaySignature: "simulated_valid_test_signature",
        }),
      });

      const verifyData = await verifyRes.json();
      if (verifyRes.ok && verifyData.success) {
        setTimeout(() => {
          clearCart();
          router.push(`/order-confirmation/${orderNumber}`);
        }, 1500);
      } else {
        throw new Error("Simulation verification failed.");
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Something went wrong during checkout.";
      setErrorMessage(msg);
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-10 sm:py-16 space-y-10">
      
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[#EBE3D8] pb-5">
        <div>
          <Link
            href="/cart"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs uppercase tracking-widest font-sans text-taupe hover:text-charcoal mb-1"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Back to Bag
          </Link>
          <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl text-charcoal">Prepaid Checkout</h1>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-sans text-taupe bg-ivory px-3 py-1.5 border border-champagne">
          <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-taupe-dark" />
          <span>256-bit Encrypted</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Mandatory Prepaid Notice */}
      <div className="bg-ivory/80 border border-champagne p-4 text-xs font-sans text-charcoal/90 space-y-1">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-medium text-charcoal">
          <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-taupe-dark shrink-0" />
          <span>Prepaid Orders Only &bull; Cash on Delivery (COD) Unavailable</span>
        </div>
        <p className="text-charcoal/70 pl-6 leading-relaxed">
          To maintain handcrafted boutique standards and preserve the pristine condition of delicate baroque pearls and ribbons, we only accept prepaid orders (UPI, NetBanking, Cards). Every parcel is sealed in a tamper-evident boutique box.
        </p>
      </div>

      {errorMessage && (
        <div className="bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 p-4 text-xs font-sans text-rose-800 flex items-center gap-2">
          <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
          <span>{errorMessage}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {demoNotice && (
        <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 p-4 text-xs font-sans text-amber-900 flex items-center gap-2">
          <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-amber-700" />
          <span>{demoNotice}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      <form onSubmit={handleCheckoutSubmit} className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-10 items-start">
        
        {/* Shipping Form (8 cols) */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-7 bg-white border border-[#EBE3D8] p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-6">
          <h2 className="font-serif text-xl text-charcoal border-b border-[#EBE3D8] pb-3">
            1. Shipping &amp; Contact Details
          </h2>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs font-sans">
            <div className="sm:col-span-2 space-y-1">
              <label className="block text-charcoal font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">
                Full Name *
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                name="name"
                required
                value={formData.name}
                onChange={handleInputChange}
                placeholder="e.g. Radhika Sen"
                className="w-full bg-ivory/30 border border-champagne p-3 text-xs text-charcoal focus:outline-none focus:border-charcoal"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1">
              <label className="block text-charcoal font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">
                Email Address *
              </label>
              <input
                type="email"
                name="email"
                required
                value={formData.email}
                onChange={handleInputChange}
                placeholder="For tracking & receipt"
                className="w-full bg-ivory/30 border border-champagne p-3 text-xs text-charcoal focus:outline-none focus:border-charcoal"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1">
              <label className="block text-charcoal font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">
                Mobile Number (+91) *
              </label>
              <input
                type="tel"
                name="phone"
                required
                value={formData.phone}
                onChange={handleInputChange}
                placeholder="10-digit mobile"
                className="w-full bg-ivory/30 border border-champagne p-3 text-xs text-charcoal focus:outline-none focus:border-charcoal"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="sm:col-span-2 space-y-1">
              <label className="block text-charcoal font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">

                type="text"

                placeholder="e.g. Flat 304, Aster Heights"
                className="w-full bg-ivory/30 border border-champagne p-3 text-xs text-charcoal focus:outline-none focus:border-charcoal"
              />
            </div>

              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                name="street"

                placeholder="e.g. 5th Cross, Lavelle Road"
                className="w-full bg-ivory/30 border border-champagne p-3 text-xs text-charcoal focus:outline-none focus:border-charcoal"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1">
              <label className="block text-charcoal font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">
                City / District *
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                name="city"

                placeholder="e.g. Bengaluru"
                className="w-full bg-ivory/30 border border-champagne p-3 text-xs text-charcoal focus:outline-none focus:border-charcoal"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1">
              <label className="block text-charcoal font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">
                PIN Code *
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                name="pincode"
                required
                maxLength={6}
                value={formData.pincode}
                onChange={handleInputChange}
                placeholder="6-digit PIN code"
                className="w-full bg-ivory/30 border border-champagne p-3 text-xs text-charcoal focus:outline-none focus:border-charcoal"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1">
              <label className="block text-charcoal font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">
                State / Union Territory *
              </label>
              <select
                name="state"
                value={formData.state}
                onChange={handleInputChange}
                className="w-full bg-ivory/30 border border-champagne p-3 text-xs text-charcoal focus:outline-none focus:border-charcoal cursor-pointer"
              >
                {INDIAN_STATES.map((s) => (
                  <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1">
              <label className="block text-charcoal font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">
                Country
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                disabled
                value="India (Domestic)"
                className="w-full bg-gray-100 border border-gray-300 p-3 text-xs text-charcoal/70 cursor-not-allowed"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="pt-4 border-t border-[#EBE3D8] space-y-2">
            <h2 className="font-serif text-xl text-charcoal">
              2. Payment Method
            </h2>
            <div className="border border-champagne bg-ivory/30 p-4 space-y-2 text-xs font-sans">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between font-medium text-charcoal">
                <span>Online Prepaid (Razorpay Secure Gateway)</span>
                <span className="text-[10px] bg-charcoal text-white px-2 py-0.5 uppercase tracking-widest">
                  Verified
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-charcoal/70 leading-relaxed text-[11px]">
                Instant UPI (Google Pay, PhonePe, Paytm), All Major Debit/Credit Cards (Visa, MasterCard, RuPay), and NetBanking.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

        {/* Order Summary & Pay Action (5 cols) */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-5 bg-white border border-[#EBE3D8] p-6 space-y-6">
          <h2 className="font-serif text-xl text-charcoal border-b border-[#EBE3D8] pb-3">
            Order Review
          </h2>

          {/* Mini items list */}
          <div className="space-y-3 max-h-72 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
            {items.map((i) => {
              const unitPrice = i.variant?.price_override ?? i.product.selling_price;
              const img = i.product.images[0]?.image_url || "";
              return (
                <div key={`${i.product.id}-${i.variant?.id}`} className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs font-sans pb-3 border-b border-[#EBE3D8]/60">
                  <div className="relative w-12 h-14 bg-ivory border border-[#EBE3D8] shrink-0 overflow-hidden">
                    {img && <Image src={img} alt={i.product.name} fill className="object-cover" />}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <p className="font-serif text-sm text-charcoal line-clamp-1">{i.product.name}</p>
                    {i.variant && <span className="text-[10px] text-taupe">{i.variant.title}</span>}
                    <p className="text-taupe">Qty: {i.quantity}</p>
                  </div>
                  <span className="font-medium text-charcoal">₹{(unitPrice * i.quantity).toLocaleString("en-IN")}</span>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-2.5 text-xs font-sans border-t border-[#EBE3D8] pt-4">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-charcoal/80">
              <span>Subtotal</span>
              <span>₹{subtotal.toLocaleString("en-IN")}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between text-charcoal/80">
              <span>Shipping Fee</span>
              <span>{shippingFee === 0 ? "FREE" : `₹${shippingFee}`}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between text-base font-medium text-charcoal pt-2 border-t border-[#EBE3D8]">
              <span>Total Payable</span>
              <span>₹{totalPayable.toLocaleString("en-IN")}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={isLoading}
            className="btn-boutique-primary w-full py-4 text-center text-xs uppercase tracking-widest disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {isLoading ? "Securing Payment Session..." : `Pay ₹${totalPayable.toLocaleString("en-IN")} & Confirm Order`}
          </button>

          <p className="text-[10px] text-center text-taupe font-sans leading-relaxed">
            By clicking Pay, you agree to Petal &amp; Pearl&apos;s Terms &amp; Conditions and Shipping Policy. All transactions are securely processed.
          </p>

        </div>

      </form>

    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";

    name: "",
    email: "",
    orderNumber: "",
    subject: "general",
    message: "",
  });

    e.preventDefault();
    setSubmitted(true);
  };

  return (

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ChevronDown, Sparkles } from "lucide-react";

interface FAQItem {
  question: string;
  answer: string;
  category: "orders" | "shipping" | "care" | "returns" | "gifting";
}

const FAQS: FAQItem[] = [
  {
    category: "orders",
    question: "Why does Petal & Pearl operate exclusively on prepaid orders?",
    answer: "We are an artisanal boutique. Every piece is curated, inspected, and packed in luxury keepsake packaging. Operating exclusively on prepaid transactions (UPI, Cards, NetBanking) allows us to dedicate resources to premium unboxing materials, avoid shipping wastage, and maintain fast, insured dispatch timelines.",
  },
  {
    category: "orders",
    question: "Which payment methods are accepted?",
    answer: "We support all major Indian digital payment modes via our 256-bit encrypted Razorpay gateway: Instant UPI (Google Pay, PhonePe, Paytm, BHIM), Debit & Credit Cards (Visa, MasterCard, RuPay), and Internet Banking from 50+ Indian banks.",
  },
  {
    category: "shipping",
    question: "What is your shipping timeline and which courier partners are used?",
    answer: "All in-stock orders are prepared and dispatched within 24–48 business hours from our studio. We partner with India's premier express couriers (BlueDart and Delhivery). Standard delivery across tier-1 and metro cities takes 3–5 business days, while other locations take 4–6 business days.",
  },
  {
    category: "shipping",
    question: "How do I qualify for complimentary shipping?",
    answer: "All orders totaling ₹999 or above automatically receive Free Express Delivery at checkout. For orders under ₹999, a flat shipping fee of ₹99 is charged to cover secure express courier handling.",
  },
  {
    category: "care",
    question: "Are your pearls natural freshwater baroque pearls?",
    answer: "Yes. We source genuine, ethically harvested freshwater baroque pearls. Because nature shapes them over months without artificial molds, each baroque pearl features its own unique organic form, subtle ridges, and natural iridescence. No two pairs are ever identical.",
  },
  {
    category: "care",
    question: "How should I care for my baroque pearl jewelry and silk hair accessories?",
    answer: "Pearls thrive on gentle care. As a golden rule: 'Last on, first off.' Put your pearls on after perfume, lotions, and hairspray have dried. To clean, gently wipe with a soft, damp cloth and store them in your Petal & Pearl velvet pouch away from abrasive metals. For silk accessories, spot clean with cold water and mild silk detergent.",
  },
  {
    category: "returns",
    question: "What is your policy if an item arrives damaged in transit?",
    answer: "We pack every order with thick cushioning inside rigid boxes. However, if your parcel arrives damaged or with a defect, contact us at hello@thepearlpetals.in within 7 days of delivery with an unboxing photo or video, and we will dispatch a brand-new replacement immediately at zero additional cost.",
  },
  {
    category: "returns",
    question: "Can I return earrings if I change my mind?",
    answer: "For hygiene and sanitation reasons in accordance with Indian consumer health standards, earrings cannot be returned or exchanged once their hygiene seal has been opened, unless there is a verifiable manufacturing defect or transit damage.",
  },
  {
    category: "gifting",
    question: "Can I send an order directly as a gift without pricing information inside?",
    answer: "Yes! All Petal & Pearl orders are packed in unbranded external mailers containing our signature embossed gift box, tissue, and champagne ribbon. We do not insert price tags inside the jewelry box. Tax invoices are sent exclusively to the purchaser's email address.",
  },
];

export default function FAQPage() {
  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState<string>("all");
  const [openIndices, setOpenIndices] = useState<number[]>([0, 2]); // First few open by default

  const toggleIndex = (idx: number) => {
    setOpenIndices((prev) =>
      prev.includes(idx) ? prev.filter((i) => i !== idx) : [...prev, idx]
    );
  };

  const filteredFaqs = activeCategory === "all"
    ? FAQS
    : FAQS.filter((f) => f.category === activeCategory);

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-12 sm:py-20 space-y-12">
      
      {/* Title */}
      <div className="text-center space-y-3">
        <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-taupe font-sans block">
          Knowledge Base &bull; Boutique Care
        </span>
        <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl sm:text-5xl text-charcoal">
          Frequently Asked Questions
        </h1>
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-charcoal/70 font-sans max-w-md mx-auto font-light">
          Everything you need to know about our handcrafted pearls, prepaid checkout, unboxing care, and express dispatch.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Filter Tabs */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2 border-b border-[#EBE3D8] pb-4">
        {[
          { label: "All Questions", value: "all" },
          { label: "Orders & Payments", value: "orders" },
          { label: "Shipping & Delivery", value: "shipping" },
          { label: "Jewelry Care", value: "care" },
          { label: "Returns & Replacements", value: "returns" },
          { label: "Gifting", value: "gifting" },
        ].map((tab) => (
          <button
            key={tab.value}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveCategory(tab.value)}
            className={`px-4 py-2 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-sans transition-all ${
              activeCategory === tab.value
                ? "bg-charcoal text-white font-medium"
                : "bg-white border border-[#EBE3D8] text-charcoal/70 hover:border-champagne hover:text-charcoal"
            }`}
          >
            {tab.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Accordion FAQ list */}
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {filteredFaqs.map((faq, idx) => {
          const isOpen = openIndices.includes(idx);
          return (
            <div
              key={faq.question}
              className="bg-white border border-[#EBE3D8] transition-all hover:border-champagne"
            >
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => toggleIndex(idx)}
                className="w-full p-5 text-left flex items-center justify-between gap-4 focus:outline-none"
              >
                <h3 className="font-serif text-lg sm:text-xl text-charcoal leading-snug">
                  {faq.question}
                </h3>
                <ChevronDown
                  className={`w-4 h-4 text-taupe shrink-0 transition-transform duration-300 ${
                    isOpen ? "rotate-180 text-charcoal" : ""
                  }`}
                />
              </button>

              {isOpen && (
                <div className="px-5 pb-5 pt-1 text-xs sm:text-sm text-charcoal/80 font-sans font-light leading-relaxed border-t border-[#EBE3D8]/50">
                  <p>{faq.answer}</p>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Bottom Help Banner */}
      <div className="bg-ivory/60 border border-champagne p-8 text-center space-y-3">
        <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5 text-taupe mx-auto" />
        <h3 className="font-serif text-xl text-charcoal">Have an unanswered question?</h3>
        <p className="text-xs text-charcoal/70 font-sans max-w-sm mx-auto font-light">
          Our boutique concierge team is always delighted to assist you directly.
        </p>
        <div className="pt-2">
          <Link href="/contact" className="btn-boutique-primary">
            Contact Concierge
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next";
import "./globals.css";

import Link from "next/link";
import Image from "next/image";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { getOrderByNumber } from "@/lib/store";
import { CheckCircle2, PackageCheck, Truck, Compass, ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";

export default async function OrderConfirmationPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ orderNumber: string }>;
}) {
  const { orderNumber } = await params;
  const order = getOrderByNumber(orderNumber);

  if (!order) {
    notFound();
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 py-16 sm:py-24 space-y-12">
      
      {/* Success Badge & Headline */}
      <div className="text-center space-y-4">
        <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto text-emerald-700">
          <CheckCircle2 className="w-8 h-8" />
        </div>
        
        <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-taupe font-sans block">
          Order Confirmed &bull; Payment Received
        </span>
        
        <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl sm:text-5xl text-charcoal">
          Thank you, {order.customer.name.split(" ")[0]}!
        </h1>
        
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-charcoal/80 font-sans max-w-lg mx-auto font-light">
          Your order <span className="font-medium text-charcoal">#{order.order_number}</span> has been securely placed. We have dispatched a confirmation receipt to <span className="font-medium text-charcoal">{order.customer.email}</span>.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Fulfillment Status Card */}
      <div className="bg-ivory/50 border border-champagne p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-6">
        <h2 className="font-serif text-xl text-charcoal border-b border-champagne/60 pb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
          <PackageCheck className="w-5 h-5 text-taupe-dark" />
          <span>Boutique Fulfillment Status</span>
        </h2>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6 text-xs font-sans">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <span className="text-taupe uppercase tracking-wider block">Order Status</span>
            <span className="font-medium text-charcoal capitalize px-2 py-0.5 bg-white border border-champagne inline-block">
              {order.status.replace("_", " ")}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1">
            <span className="text-taupe uppercase tracking-wider block">Payment Verification</span>
            <span className="font-medium text-emerald-800">
              Verified &amp; Paid (Online Prepaid)
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1">
            <span className="text-taupe uppercase tracking-wider block">Estimated Dispatch</span>
            <span className="font-medium text-charcoal">
              Within 24-48 business hours
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white p-4 border border-[#EBE3D8] text-xs font-sans text-charcoal/80 space-y-2">
          <p className="flex items-center gap-2 font-medium text-charcoal">
            <Truck className="w-4 h-4 text-taupe-dark" />
            What happens next?
          </p>
          <p className="text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-charcoal/70">
            Our boutique artisans are now assembling your order with gentle care. Each baroque pearl is checked for luster and structural integrity, placed into our rigid keepsake packaging, and handed over to BlueDart or Delhivery express.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Order Itemized Summary */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-[#EBE3D8] p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-6">
        <h2 className="font-serif text-xl text-charcoal border-b border-[#EBE3D8] pb-3">
          Items in this Parcel
        </h2>

        <div className="space-y-4">
          {order.items.map((item) => (
            <div key={item.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-4 border-b border-[#EBE3D8]/60 text-xs font-sans">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                <div className="relative w-16 h-20 bg-ivory border border-[#EBE3D8] shrink-0 overflow-hidden">
                  {item.image_url && <Image src={item.image_url} alt={item.product_name} fill className="object-cover" />}
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="font-serif text-base text-charcoal">{item.product_name}</h3>
                  {item.variant_title && <p className="text-taupe">{item.variant_title}</p>}
                  <p className="text-taupe">Quantity: {item.quantity}</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <span className="font-medium text-charcoal text-sm">
                ₹{item.total_price.toLocaleString("en-IN")}
              </span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Pricing totals */}
        <div className="space-y-2 text-xs font-sans pt-2 max-w-xs ml-auto">
          <div className="flex justify-between text-charcoal/70">
            <span>Subtotal:</span>
            <span>₹{order.subtotal.toLocaleString("en-IN")}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex justify-between text-charcoal/70">
            <span>Shipping:</span>
            <span>{order.shipping_fee === 0 ? "FREE" : `₹${order.shipping_fee}`}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex justify-between text-base font-medium text-charcoal pt-2 border-t border-[#EBE3D8]">
            <span>Total Paid:</span>
            <span>₹{order.total_payable.toLocaleString("en-IN")}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Delivery Address */}
        <div className="border-t border-[#EBE3D8] pt-6 text-xs font-sans space-y-1">
          <span className="text-taupe uppercase tracking-wider block font-medium">
            Delivery Address:
          </span>
          <p className="text-charcoal font-medium">{order.customer.name}</p>
          <p className="text-charcoal/80">{order.customer.house}, {order.customer.street}</p>
          <p className="text-charcoal/80">{order.customer.city}, {order.customer.state} - {order.customer.pincode}</p>
          <p className="text-charcoal/80">Phone: {order.customer.phone}</p>
        </div>

      </div>

      {/* Action Buttons */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-center gap-4 pt-4">
        <Link
          href={`/track-order?orderNumber=${order.order_number}`}
          className="btn-boutique-secondary w-full sm:w-auto px-6 py-3.5 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
        >
          <Compass className="w-4 h-4 text-taupe" />
          <span>Track Live Status</span>
        </Link>
        <Link
          href="/shop"
          className="btn-boutique-primary w-full sm:w-auto px-6 py-3.5 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
        >
          <span>Continue Shopping</span>
          <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
        </Link>
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

export default function HomePage() {
  const [allProducts, setAllProducts] = useState<PublicProduct[]>([]);
  const [loadingProducts, setLoadingProducts] = useState(true);

  // VIP Drop List Form State
  const [vipEmail, setVipEmail] = useState("");
  const [submittingVip, setSubmittingVip] = useState(false);
  const [vipMessage, setVipMessage] = useState("");
  const [vipSuccess, setVipSuccess] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/products")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (data?.products && Array.isArray(data.products)) {
          setAllProducts(data.products);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {})
      .finally(() => setLoadingProducts(false));
  }, []);

  const handleVipSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!vipEmail || !vipEmail.includes("@")) return;

    setSubmittingVip(true);
    setVipMessage("");

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/newsletter", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email: vipEmail }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) {
        setVipSuccess(true);
        setVipMessage(data.message || "You are on the VIP Close Friends list! Welcome code WELCOME10 is saved.");
        setVipEmail("");
      } else {
        setVipMessage(data.error || "Could not register email. Please try again.");
      }
    } catch {
      setVipMessage("Network error. Please try again.");
    } finally {
      setSubmittingVip(false);
    }
  };

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<string>("all");

  const hottestPicks = allProducts.filter((p) => p.is_bestseller);
  const hairProducts = allProducts.filter((p) => p.category === "hair-accessories");
  const jewelleryProducts = allProducts.filter((p) => p.category === "jewellery");
  const essentialsProducts = allProducts.filter((p) => p.category === "everyday-essentials");
  const giftProducts = allProducts.filter((p) => p.category === "gifts");
  const featured = allProducts.filter((p) => p.is_featured);

        <div className="animate-marquee flex items-center gap-8 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-charcoal/85 font-sans">
          <span>✦ THAT GIRL AESTHETIC</span>
          <span className="text-champagne-dark">•</span>
          <span>WATERPROOF &amp; TARNISH-FREE</span>
          <span className="text-champagne-dark">•</span>

          <span className="text-champagne-dark">•</span>

              Every parcel is treated as a gift to yourself.
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-charcoal/70 font-sans leading-relaxed font-light">
              We never pack your orders in plain plastic mailers. Every single Petal &amp; Pearl creation is cushioned inside an embossed rigid drawer box, enveloped in botanical tissue, and tied with a champagne grosgrain bow. Complete with care guidance and a handwritten thank you card.
            </p>
            <div className="pt-2">
              <Link href="/category/gifts" className="btn-boutique-primary text-xs">
                Explore Gift Sets &rarr;
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="md:col-span-5 relative aspect-[4/3] overflow-hidden border border-champagne shadow-sm bg-white">
            <Image
              src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1549465220-1a8b9238cd48?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80"
              alt="Petal & Pearl gift packaging presentation"
              fill
              className="object-cover object-center"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

    </div>
  );
}

            <li>Generating your digital order confirmation and tax invoice.</li>

            <li>Providing order tracking updates via SMS and email.</li>

          <div className="bg-ivory/60 border border-champagne p-4 not-prose text-xs text-charcoal/80 space-y-1 font-sans">
            <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal flex items-center gap-1.5">

              We will never sell, rent, monetize, or license your contact details, purchase history, or personal profile to third-party advertising brokers, lead generation firms, or marketing agencies.
