// ==============================================================================
// FILE: src/app/product/[slug]/page.tsx
// ==============================================================================

"use client";

import { use, useState, useEffect } from "react";
import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { getPublicProductBySlug, getPublicProducts } from "@/lib/public-catalog";
import { PublicProduct } from "@/lib/types";
import { useCart } from "@/context/cart-context";
import ProductCard from "@/components/ProductCard";
import {
  Check,
  Truck,
  RotateCcw,
  ShieldCheck,
  Minus,
  Plus,
  ShoppingBag,
  Sparkles,
  Heart,
  Share2,
  ExternalLink,
} from "lucide-react";

export default function ProductDetailPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
  const { slug } = use(params);
  const router = useRouter();
  const initialProduct = getPublicProductBySlug(slug);
  const [product, setProduct] = useState<PublicProduct | null>(initialProduct);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(!initialProduct);

  const { addItem } = useCart();
  const [selectedImageIdx, setSelectedImageIdx] = useState(0);
  const [selectedVariant, setSelectedVariant] = useState(initialProduct?.variants[0] || null);
  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState(1);
  const [addedAnimation, setAddedAnimation] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!product) {
      fetch(`/api/products/${encodeURIComponent(slug)}`)
        .then((res) => res.json())
        .then((data) => {
          if (data?.product) {
            setProduct(data.product);
            if (data.product.variants?.[0]) {
              setSelectedVariant(data.product.variants[0]);
            }
          }
        })
        .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    }
  }, [slug, product]);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 py-28 text-center space-y-3 font-sans">
        <p className="font-serif text-2xl text-charcoal">Loading boutique piece...</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-taupe">Retrieving piece details from the collection</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!product) {
    notFound();
  }

  const images = product.images.length > 0
    ? product.images
    : [{ id: "fallback", image_url: "", alt_text: product.name, display_order: 1 }];

  const [relatedProducts, setRelatedProducts] = useState<PublicProduct[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (product) {
      fetch(`/api/products?category=${encodeURIComponent(product.category)}`)
        .then((res) => res.json())
        .then((data) => {
          if (data?.products && Array.isArray(data.products)) {
            setRelatedProducts(data.products.filter((p: PublicProduct) => p.id !== product.id).slice(0, 4));
          }
        })
        .catch(() => {});
    }
  }, [product]);

  const currentPrice = selectedVariant?.price_override ?? product?.selling_price ?? 0;
  const currentStock = selectedVariant ? selectedVariant.stock : (product?.stock ?? 0);

  const handleAddToBag = () => {
    if (currentStock <= 0) return;
    addItem(product, selectedVariant || undefined, quantity);
    setAddedAnimation(true);
    setTimeout(() => setAddedAnimation(false), 2000);
  };

  const handleBuyNow = () => {
    if (currentStock <= 0) return;
    addItem(product, selectedVariant || undefined, quantity);
    router.push("/checkout");
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-10 sm:py-16 space-y-16">
      
      {/* Breadcrumbs */}
      <nav className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-sans text-taupe">
        <Link href="/" className="hover:text-charcoal transition-colors">Home</Link>
        <span>/</span>
        <Link href={`/category/${product.category}`} className="hover:text-charcoal transition-colors capitalize">
          {product.category.replace("-", " ")}
        </Link>
        <span>/</span>
        <span className="text-charcoal font-medium truncate max-w-xs">{product.name}</span>
      </nav>

      {/* Main Product Layout */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-10 lg:gap-14 items-start">
        
        {/* Left Column: Image Gallery */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-7 flex flex-col-reverse sm:flex-row gap-4">
          
          {/* Thumbnails (desktop left, mobile bottom) */}
          {images.length > 1 && (
            <div className="flex sm:flex-col gap-3 overflow-x-auto sm:overflow-visible shrink-0 pb-2 sm:pb-0">
              {images.map((img, idx) => (
                <button
                  key={img.id || idx}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSelectedImageIdx(idx)}
                  className={`relative w-16 h-20 sm:w-20 sm:h-24 bg-ivory border transition-all overflow-hidden shrink-0 ${
                    selectedImageIdx === idx ? "border-charcoal ring-1 ring-charcoal" : "border-[#EBE3D8] hover:border-champagne"
                  }`}
                >
                  <Image
                    src={img.image_url}
                    alt={img.alt_text || product.name}
                    fill
                    unoptimized
                    referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                    onError={(e) => {
                      const target = e.target as HTMLImageElement;
                      target.srcset = "";
                      target.src = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1535632066927-ab7c9ab60908?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80";
                    }}
                    className="object-cover object-center"
                  />
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Main Display Image */}
          <div className="relative aspect-[4/5] flex-1 bg-ivory/40 border border-[#EBE3D8] overflow-hidden">
            {images[selectedImageIdx] && (
              <Image
                src={images[selectedImageIdx].image_url}
                alt={images[selectedImageIdx].alt_text || product.name}
                fill
                priority
                unoptimized
                referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                onError={(e) => {
                  const target = e.target as HTMLImageElement;
                  target.srcset = "";
                  target.src = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1535632066927-ab7c9ab60908?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80";
                }}
                className="object-cover object-center transition-all duration-500"
              />
            )}

            {/* Badges */}
            <div className="absolute top-4 left-4 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
              {product.is_bestseller && (
                <span className="bg-[#2D2A26] text-[#FAF8F5] text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider px-3 py-1 font-sans font-semibold shadow-sm">
                  🔥 Hottest Pick
                </span>
              )}
              {product.is_new && !product.is_bestseller && (
                <span className="bg-ivory text-charcoal text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest px-3 py-1 font-sans font-medium border border-champagne">
                  ✦ New Drop
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

        {/* Right Column: Product Details & Actions */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-5 space-y-6">
          
          {/* Header & Price */}
          <div className="space-y-2 border-b border-[#EBE3D8] pb-6">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-taupe font-sans block">
                {product.subcategory.replace("-", " ")} &bull; SKU: {selectedVariant?.sku || product.sku}
              </span>
              {(product.product_link || product.source_url) && (
                <a
                  href={product.product_link || product.source_url}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-sans text-taupe hover:text-charcoal transition-colors underline"
                  title="View original product source"
                >
                  <span>Source Reference</span>
                  <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
                </a>
              )}
            </div>
            <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl sm:text-4xl text-charcoal leading-snug">
              {product.name}
            </h1>
            <div className="pt-2 flex items-baseline gap-3">
              <span className="text-2xl font-serif text-charcoal font-medium">
                ₹{currentPrice.toLocaleString("en-IN")}
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs font-sans text-taupe font-light">
                Inclusive of all taxes &bull; Prepaid only
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Product Description */}
          <div className="prose prose-sm text-charcoal/80 font-sans font-light leading-relaxed">
            <p>{product.description}</p>
          </div>

          {/* Variants Selector */}
          {product.variants.length > 1 && (
            <div className="space-y-2.5 pt-2">
              <label className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-sans font-medium text-charcoal block">
                Select Option: <span className="font-normal text-taupe">{selectedVariant?.title}</span>
              </label>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2.5">
                {product.variants.map((v) => {
                  const isSelected = selectedVariant?.id === v.id;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={v.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setSelectedVariant(v)}
                      className={`px-4 py-2 text-xs font-sans transition-all border ${
                        isSelected
                          ? "bg-charcoal text-white border-charcoal font-medium shadow-sm"
                          : "bg-white text-charcoal/80 border-[#EBE3D8] hover:border-champagne"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {v.title}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Stock Status Indicator */}
          <div className="pt-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-sans">
            {currentStock > 5 ? (
              <span className="text-emerald-800 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-600 animate-pulse" />
                In Stock &amp; Ready for Immediate Dispatch
              </span>
            ) : currentStock > 0 ? (
              <span className="text-amber-800 flex items-center gap-1.5 font-medium">
                <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-amber-600" />
                Only {currentStock} handcrafted pieces left in batch
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span className="text-rose-700 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-rose-600" />
                Currently Out of Stock &bull; Small Batch Restocking Soon
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Quantity & CTA Buttons */}
          <div className="space-y-3 pt-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              {/* Quantity Selector */}
              <div className="flex items-center border border-champagne bg-white h-12">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setQuantity(Math.max(1, quantity - 1))}
                  className="px-3.5 h-full text-charcoal hover:bg-ivory transition-colors"
                  aria-label="Decrease quantity"
                >
                  <Minus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </button>
                <span className="px-4 text-xs font-sans font-medium text-charcoal">
                  {quantity}
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setQuantity(Math.min(currentStock, quantity + 1))}
                  disabled={quantity >= currentStock}
                  className="px-3.5 h-full text-charcoal hover:bg-ivory transition-colors disabled:opacity-30"
                  aria-label="Increase quantity"
                >
                  <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Add to Bag */}
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleAddToBag}
                disabled={currentStock <= 0}
                className="btn-boutique-primary flex-1 h-12 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
              >
                {addedAnimation ? (
                  <>
                    <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-champagne" />
                    <span>Added to Bag!</span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <ShoppingBag className="w-4 h-4" />
                    <span>Add to Bag</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Direct Buy Now */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleBuyNow}
              disabled={currentStock <= 0}
              className="btn-boutique-champagne w-full h-12 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              <span>Buy Now with Instant Checkout</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Delivery & Care Trust Accordion */}
          <div className="border-t border-[#EBE3D8] pt-6 space-y-4 text-xs font-sans">
            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
              <Truck className="w-4 h-4 text-taupe shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <div>
                <p className="font-medium text-charcoal">Express Shipping Across India</p>
                <p className="text-charcoal/70 mt-0.5 font-light">
                  Orders dispatched within 24-48 hours via BlueDart / Delhivery. Estimated delivery 4-6 business days. Free on orders above ₹999.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
              <RotateCcw className="w-4 h-4 text-taupe shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <div>
                <p className="font-medium text-charcoal">7-Day Replacement Guarantee</p>
                <p className="text-charcoal/70 mt-0.5 font-light">
                  In the rare event of transit damage or defect, we offer hassle-free replacements. Due to personal hygiene considerations, earrings are non-returnable once seal is broken.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-taupe shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <div>
                <p className="font-medium text-charcoal">100% Prepaid Verification</p>
                <p className="text-charcoal/70 mt-0.5 font-light">
                  We operate exclusively on prepaid orders to guarantee dedicated boutique gift packaging and priority dispatch.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

      </div>

      {/* Related Products Carousel/Grid */}
      {relatedProducts.length > 0 && (
        <section className="pt-12 border-t border-[#EBE3D8] space-y-8">
          <div className="text-center space-y-1">
            <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-taupe font-sans">
              Pair With Delicate Pieces
            </span>
            <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-charcoal">
              You May Also Adore
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-5 sm:gap-6">
            {relatedProducts.map((p) => (
              <ProductCard key={p.id} product={p} />
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}

// FILE: src/app/returns-policy/page.tsx

export default function ReturnsPolicyPage() {
  return (

      {/* Title */}

          Boutique Assurance

      <div className="prose prose-sm max-w-none text-charcoal/80 font-sans font-light leading-relaxed space-y-8">

        <div className="bg-amber-50/70 border border-amber-200 p-6 space-y-2 not-prose">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-amber-900 font-medium text-xs uppercase tracking-wider">
            <ShieldAlert className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-700 shrink-0" />
            <span>Important Notice Concerning Hygiene &amp; Personal Adornment</span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-amber-900/80 leading-relaxed font-sans">
            In compliance with Indian public health and sanitary standards, earrings, piercings, and personal hair accessories are classified as personal hygiene products. Once their protective packaging seal is broken, they cannot be returned for change of mind. We gladly replace any piece that arrives damaged or with a defect.
          </p>
        </div>

        <section className="space-y-3">
          <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl text-charcoal font-normal">1. 7-Day Replacement Guarantee</h2>
          <p>
            We take tremendous care in packing our delicate baroque pearls and accessories. However, if your order arrives damaged, defective, or incorrect, you are entitled to a complimentary replacement under our 7-Day Replacement Guarantee.
          </p>
          <p>
            <strong>Eligibility Criteria:</strong>
          </p>
          <ul className="list-disc pl-5 space-y-1 text-xs sm:text-sm">
            <li>The issue must be reported to <a href="mailto:hello@thepearlpetals.in" className="underline text-charcoal">hello@thepearlpetals.in</a> within 7 calendar days of delivery.</li>
            <li>The item must be in its original condition, unworn, with all tags intact, and housed in its original Petal &amp; Pearl keepsake box.</li>
            <li>A clear unboxing photograph or short video showing the damage, defective clasp, or incorrect item must be provided.</li>
          </ul>
        </section>

        <section className="space-y-3">
          <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl text-charcoal font-normal">2. How to Request a Replacement</h2>
          <ol className="list-decimal pl-5 space-y-2 text-xs sm:text-sm">
            <li>Email our concierge at <a href="mailto:hello@thepearlpetals.in" className="underline text-charcoal font-medium">hello@thepearlpetals.in</a> with the subject line <code>Replacement Request - [Your Order Number]</code>.</li>
            <li>Attach photos/video highlighting the defect along with a brief description.</li>
            <li>Our quality assessment team will review your request within 24 business hours.</li>
            <li>Upon approval, we will schedule a reverse pickup through BlueDart/Delhivery or dispatch a brand-new replacement immediately at zero extra charge.</li>
          </ol>
        </section>

        <section className="space-y-3">
          <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl text-charcoal font-normal">3. Refund Processing &amp; Timelines</h2>
          <p>
            If a replacement cannot be provided due to batch stock unavailability or permanent discontinuation of the piece, a full refund will be issued:
          </p>
          <ul className="list-disc pl-5 space-y-1 text-xs sm:text-sm">
            <li>
              <strong>Source Account Refund:</strong> The refund is credited directly to the original payment source (UPI account, Credit/Debit Card, or Bank Account used at checkout).
            </li>
            <li>
              <strong>Settlement Timeline:</strong> Refunds are initiated by our finance desk within 48 hours of approval. Depending on your bank or card network, the funds will reflect in your account within <strong>5–7 business days</strong>.
            </li>
          </ul>
        </section>

        <section className="space-y-3">
          <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl text-charcoal font-normal">4. Non-Returnable Scenarios</h2>
          <ul className="list-disc pl-5 space-y-1 text-xs sm:text-sm">
            <li>Items returned without original packaging, tags, or evidence of having been worn.</li>
            <li>Products damaged due to misuse, contact with chemical perfumes/hair dyes, or improper storage outside our care guidelines.</li>
            <li>Requests initiated after the expiry of the 7-day delivery window.</li>
          </ul>
        </section>

        <section className="space-y-3 border-t border-[#EBE3D8] pt-6">
          <h2 className="font-serif text-xl text-charcoal font-normal">Need Guidance?</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-charcoal/70">
            For any queries regarding replacement status or product care, write to us anytime at{" "}
            <a href="mailto:hello@thepearlpetals.in" className="underline text-charcoal font-medium">hello@thepearlpetals.in</a>.
          </p>
        </section>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}


// ==============================================================================
// FILE: src/app/search/page.tsx
// ==============================================================================

"use client";

import { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from "react";
import { Search as SearchIcon, X, Sparkles } from "lucide-react";
import ProductCard from "@/components/ProductCard";
import { getPublicProducts } from "@/lib/public-catalog";

export default function SearchPage() {
  const [allProducts, setAllProducts] = useState(getPublicProducts());

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/products")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (data?.products && Array.isArray(data.products) && data.products.length > 0) {
          setAllProducts(data.products);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    if (!query.trim()) return [];
    const q = query.toLowerCase().trim();
    return allProducts.filter(
      (p) =>
        p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        p.description.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        p.category.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        p.subcategory.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        p.tags.some((t) => t.toLowerCase().includes(q))
    );
  }, [allProducts, query]);

  const recommended = useMemo(
    () => allProducts.filter((p) => p.is_bestseller || p.is_featured).slice(0, 4),
    [allProducts]
  );

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-12 sm:py-16 space-y-12">
      
      {/* Search Header & Input */}
      <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto text-center space-y-6">
        <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-taupe font-sans block">
          Boutique Discovery
        </span>
        <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl sm:text-4xl text-charcoal">
          Find Your Next Keepsake
        </h1>

        <div className="relative max-w-xl mx-auto">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={query}
            onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search by pearl, choker, hair bow, phone charm, earrings..."
            autoFocus
            className="w-full bg-white border border-champagne px-5 py-4 pl-12 pr-12 text-sm text-charcoal placeholder:text-taupe focus:outline-none focus:border-charcoal shadow-sm font-sans"
          />
          <SearchIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-taupe absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
          {query && (
            <button
              onClick={() => setQuery("")}
              className="absolute right-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-taupe hover:text-charcoal p-1"
              aria-label="Clear search"
            >
              <X className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Quick Search Tag Suggestions */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2 text-xs font-sans text-taupe">
          <span>Popular searches:</span>
          {["Baroque Pearl", "Choker", "Claw Clip", "Silk Bow", "Gift Box", "Phone Charm"].map((tag) => (
            <button
              key={tag}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setQuery(tag)}
              className="bg-white border border-[#EBE3D8] px-3 py-1 text-charcoal/80 hover:border-champagne hover:text-charcoal transition-colors"
            >
              {tag}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Results Section */}
      {query.trim() ? (
        <div className="space-y-6">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs font-sans text-taupe border-b border-[#EBE3D8] pb-3">
            <span>
              Showing {filtered.length} {filtered.length === 1 ? "result" : "results"} for &ldquo;{query}&rdquo;
            </span>
          </div>

          {filtered.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-16 bg-white border border-[#EBE3D8] p-8 space-y-4">
              <Sparkles className="w-8 h-8 text-taupe mx-auto" />
              <h3 className="font-serif text-2xl text-charcoal">No exact matches found</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-charcoal/70 font-sans max-w-sm mx-auto">
                We couldn&apos;t find any items matching &ldquo;{query}&rdquo;. Try another search or explore our most-loved pieces below.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-5 sm:gap-6">
              {filtered.map((product) => (
                <ProductCard key={product.id} product={product} />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : recommended.length > 0 ? (
        /* Default State when no query is typed */
        <div className="space-y-8 pt-6">
          <div className="text-center">
            <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-taupe font-sans block mb-1">
              Curated Recommendations
            </span>
            <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl text-charcoal">Trending in the Boutique</h2>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-5 sm:gap-6">
            {recommended.map((product) => (
              <ProductCard key={product.id} product={product} />
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="text-center py-12 text-xs font-sans text-taupe">
          Start typing above to search pieces across the boutique.
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}

// ==============================================================================
// FILE: src/app/shop/page.tsx
// ==============================================================================

"use client";

import { useState, useMemo, useEffect, Suspense } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import ProductCard from "@/components/ProductCard";
import { PublicProduct } from "@/lib/types";
import { ArrowUpDown, Filter, Sparkles, X } from "lucide-react";

function ShopContent() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const [allProducts, setAllProducts] = useState<PublicProduct[]>([]);

  // Filter state
  const [activeFilter, setActiveFilter] = useState<string>("all");
  const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState<string>("relevance");
  const [onlyInStock, setOnlyInStock] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Sync initial query params
  useEffect(() => {
    const filterParam = searchParams.get("filter");
    const categoryParam = searchParams.get("category");
    const subcategoryParam = searchParams.get("subcategory");
    const sortParam = searchParams.get("sort");

    if (filterParam === "hottest") {
      setActiveFilter("hottest");
    } else if (subcategoryParam) {
      setActiveFilter(`sub:${subcategoryParam}`);
    } else if (categoryParam) {
      setActiveFilter(`cat:${categoryParam}`);
    }

    if (sortParam) {
      setSortBy(sortParam);
    }
  }, [searchParams]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/products")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (data?.products && Array.isArray(data.products)) {
          setAllProducts(data.products);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  // Compute counts for pills
  const counts = useMemo(() => {
    return {
      all: allProducts.length,
      hottest: allProducts.filter((p) => p.is_bestseller).length,
      clawClips: allProducts.filter((p) => p.subcategory === "claw-clips").length,
      hairBows: allProducts.filter((p) => p.subcategory === "hair-bows").length,
      scrunchies: allProducts.filter((p) => p.subcategory === "scrunchies").length,
      earrings: allProducts.filter((p) => p.subcategory === "earrings").length,
      necklaces: allProducts.filter((p) => p.subcategory === "necklaces").length,
      braceletsRings: allProducts.filter((p) => p.category === "jewellery" && (p.subcategory === "bracelets" || p.subcategory === "rings")).length,
      everyday: allProducts.filter((p) => p.category === "everyday-essentials").length,
      gifts: allProducts.filter((p) => p.category === "gifts").length,
    };
  }, [allProducts]);

  const filterOptions = [
    { id: "all", label: "All Items", count: counts.all },
    { id: "hottest", label: "🔥 Hottest Picks", count: counts.hottest, isHighlight: true },
    { id: "sub:claw-clips", label: "Claw Clips", count: counts.clawClips },
    { id: "sub:hair-bows", label: "Hair Bows", count: counts.hairBows },
    { id: "sub:scrunchies", label: "Scrunchies", count: counts.scrunchies },
    { id: "sub:earrings", label: "Earrings", count: counts.earrings },
    { id: "sub:necklaces", label: "Necklaces", count: counts.necklaces },
    { id: "cat:jewellery", label: "All Jewellery", count: counts.earrings + counts.necklaces + counts.braceletsRings },
    { id: "cat:everyday-essentials", label: "Everyday Charms", count: counts.everyday },
    { id: "cat:gifts", label: "Gift Sets", count: counts.gifts },
  ];

  const filteredProducts = useMemo(() => {
    let result = [...allProducts];

    // Apply Active Filter
    if (activeFilter === "hottest") {
      result = result.filter((p) => p.is_bestseller);
    } else if (activeFilter.startsWith("sub:")) {
      const sub = activeFilter.replace("sub:", "");
      result = result.filter((p) => p.subcategory === sub);
    } else if (activeFilter.startsWith("cat:")) {
      const cat = activeFilter.replace("cat:", "");
      result = result.filter((p) => p.category === cat);
    }

    if (onlyInStock) {
      result = result.filter((p) => p.stock > 0);
    }

    // Sorting
    switch (sortBy) {
      case "price-asc":
        result.sort((a, b) => a.selling_price - b.selling_price);
        break;
      case "price-desc":
        result.sort((a, b) => b.selling_price - a.selling_price);
        break;
      case "hottest":
        result.sort((a, b) => (b.is_bestseller ? 1 : 0) - (a.is_bestseller ? 1 : 0));
        break;
      case "newest":
        result.sort((a, b) => (b.is_new ? 1 : 0) - (a.is_new ? 1 : 0));
        break;
      case "alpha":
        result.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
        break;
      case "relevance":
      default:
        // Smart boutique relevance: Bestsellers -> Featured -> New
        result.sort((a, b) => {
          const scoreB = (b.is_bestseller ? 4 : 0) + (b.is_featured ? 2 : 0) + (b.is_new ? 1 : 0);
          const scoreA = (a.is_bestseller ? 4 : 0) + (a.is_featured ? 2 : 0) + (a.is_new ? 1 : 0);
          return scoreB - scoreA;
        });
        break;
    }

    return result;
  }, [allProducts, activeFilter, sortBy, onlyInStock]);

  const activeFilterLabel = filterOptions.find((f) => f.id === activeFilter)?.label;

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-12 sm:py-16 space-y-8">
      
      {/* Editorial Header */}
      <div className="text-center max-w-2xl mx-auto space-y-3">
        <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-taupe font-sans block">
          The Full Boutique Catalogue
        </span>
        <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl sm:text-5xl text-charcoal">
          Shop All Accessories
        </h1>
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-charcoal/70 font-sans font-light">
          Explore handcrafted baroque pearls, French silk ribbon bows, durable cellulose claw clips, and keepsake sets.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Filter and Sort Bar */}
      <div className="border-y border-[#EBE3D8] py-4 space-y-4">
        
        {/* Category & Section Pill Tabs */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {filterOptions.map((opt) => {
            const isSelected = activeFilter === opt.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={opt.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveFilter(opt.id)}
                className={`px-3.5 py-2 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-sans transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                  isSelected
                    ? opt.isHighlight
                      ? "bg-[#2D2A26] text-[#FAF8F5] font-medium shadow-sm"
                      : "bg-charcoal text-white font-medium shadow-sm"
                    : opt.isHighlight
                    ? "bg-[#FAF7F2] border border-champagne text-[#C2714F] hover:border-charcoal font-medium"
                    : "bg-white border border-[#EBE3D8] text-charcoal/75 hover:border-champagne hover:text-charcoal"
                }`}
              >
                <span>{opt.label}</span>
                {opt.count > 0 && (
                  <span className={`text-[10px] ${isSelected ? "opacity-80" : "text-taupe"}`}>
                    ({opt.count})
                  </span>
                )}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Sort & Stock Controls Row */}
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pt-2 border-t border-[#EBE3D8]/60 text-xs font-sans text-charcoal/80">
          
          {/* Active Filter Indicator */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span>
              Showing <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal">{filteredProducts.length}</span> pieces
            </span>
            {activeFilter !== "all" && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-ivory px-2.5 py-1 border border-champagne text-[11px] text-charcoal">
                <span>Filter: {activeFilterLabel}</span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setActiveFilter("all")}
                  className="hover:text-taupe-dark ml-1"
                  title="Clear filter"
                >
                  <X className="w-3 h-3" />
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Right Controls: In Stock & Sorting */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-5 w-full sm:w-auto justify-between sm:justify-end">
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={onlyInStock}
                onChange={(e) => setOnlyInStock(e.target.checked)}
                className="accent-charcoal rounded-none w-3.5 h-3.5 cursor-pointer"
              />
              <span>In Stock Only</span>
            </label>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-l border-[#EBE3D8] pl-4">
              <ArrowUpDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-taupe" />
              <label htmlFor="shop-sort" className="sr-only">
                Sort products by
              </label>
              <select
                id="shop-sort"
                value={sortBy}
                onChange={(e) => setSortBy(e.target.value)}
                className="bg-transparent text-charcoal text-xs uppercase tracking-wider focus:outline-none cursor-pointer py-1"
              >
                <option value="relevance">Relevance &bull; Curated</option>
                <option value="price-asc">Price: Low to High (₹)</option>
                <option value="price-desc">Price: High to Low (₹)</option>
                <option value="hottest">🔥 Hottest Picks First</option>
                <option value="newest">New Arrivals</option>
                <option value="alpha">Alphabetical (A - Z)</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

      </div>

      {/* Products Grid */}
      {allProducts.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 bg-ivory/60 border border-champagne p-8 sm:p-14 space-y-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto shadow-sm">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1 bg-white border border-champagne text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-charcoal font-sans">
            <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-taupe-dark" />
            <span>DROP 01 CURATION IN PROGRESS</span>
          </div>
          <h3 className="font-serif text-3xl sm:text-4xl text-charcoal">
            The Vault is Opening Soon
          </h3>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-charcoal/70 font-sans leading-relaxed max-w-md mx-auto">
            We are curating hand-selected freshwater baroque pearls, tarnish-free gold essentials, and French atelier hair pieces. Every item is verified for luxury finish and organic luster.
          </p>
          <div className="pt-2 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-center gap-3">
            <a
              href="/"
              className="btn-boutique-primary w-full sm:w-auto"
            >
              Explore Boutique Vibes
            </a>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : filteredProducts.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 bg-white border border-[#EBE3D8] p-8 space-y-4">
          <Filter className="w-8 h-8 text-taupe mx-auto" />
          <h3 className="font-serif text-2xl text-charcoal">No pieces found in this view</h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-charcoal/60 font-sans max-w-sm mx-auto">
            Try choosing a different section or resetting your active filters to explore our full boutique collection.
          </p>
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              setActiveFilter("all");
              setOnlyInStock(false);
            }}
            className="btn-boutique-primary mt-2"
          >
            Show All Items
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-5 sm:gap-6">
          {filteredProducts.map((product) => (
            <ProductCard key={product.id} product={product} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}

export default function ShopAllPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense
      fallback={
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 py-20 text-center text-taupe text-xs font-sans">
          Loading boutique catalogue...
        </div>
      }
    >
      <ShopContent />
    </Suspense>
  );
}


// ==============================================================================
// FILE: src/app/terms/page.tsx
// ==============================================================================

import Link from "next/link";

export default function TermsPage() {
  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-12 sm:py-20 space-y-12">
      
      {/* Title */}
      <div className="border-b border-[#EBE3D8] pb-6">
        <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-taupe font-sans block mb-1">
          Legal Agreement &bull; Boutique Terms
        </span>
        <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl sm:text-5xl text-charcoal">
          Terms &amp; Conditions
        </h1>
        <p className="text-xs text-taupe font-sans mt-2">
          Effective Date: February 2026 &bull; Domain: thepearlpetals.in
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="prose prose-sm max-w-none text-charcoal/80 font-sans font-light leading-relaxed space-y-8">
        
        <p>
          Welcome to Petal &amp; Pearl. By accessing or purchasing from our boutique website at <strong>https://thepearlpetals.in</strong>, you agree to be bound by the following Terms and Conditions. Please review them mindfully before completing an order.
        </p>

        <section className="space-y-3">
          <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl text-charcoal font-normal">1. Brand Identity &amp; Authorized Use</h2>
          <p>
            &ldquo;Petal &amp; Pearl&rdquo; and the domain <code>thepearlpetals.in</code> operate as an independent boutique brand specializing in fine fashion jewelry, baroque pearls, and curated hair adornments. All editorial copywriting, photography, graphics, logos, and digital designs are the intellectual property of Petal &amp; Pearl and protected under Indian and international copyright laws.
          </p>
        </section>

        <section className="space-y-3">
          <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl text-charcoal font-normal">2. Organic Product Descriptions &amp; Natural Variations</h2>
          <p>
            We strive to display our products with the utmost visual fidelity. However, because our creations incorporate genuine natural freshwater baroque pearls, organic acetate, and hand-cut silk velvets, subtle variations in texture, iridescence, contour, and size are an intrinsic mark of natural beauty and authentic handcrafting. These natural nuances are not considered manufacturing defects.
          </p>

        <section className="space-y-3">
          <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl text-charcoal font-normal">3. Pricing, Orders &amp; Prepaid Policy</h2>
          <ul className="list-disc pl-5 space-y-1 text-xs sm:text-sm">
            <li>All prices listed on the website are in Indian Rupees (INR ₹) and are inclusive of applicable taxes.</li>

        </section>

        <section className="space-y-3">

Detailed logistics timelines are governed by our <Link href="/shipping-policy" className="underline text-charcoal">Shipping Policy</Link>.

          <p>

Specifically, earrings and pierced accessories are exempt from returns due to sanitary regulations once packaging seals are opened.
          </p>
        </section>

        <section className="space-y-3">

            These Terms and Conditions shall be governed by and construed in accordance with the laws of the Republic of India. Any disputes arising in connection with orders placed on <code>thepearlpetals.in</code> shall be subject to the exclusive jurisdiction of the competent courts in India.
          </p>
        </section>

        <section className="space-y-3 border-t border-[#EBE3D8] pt-6">
          <h2 className="font-serif text-xl text-charcoal font-normal">Contact Inquiries</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-charcoal/70">
            For questions regarding these Terms, contact our boutique desk at{" "}
            <a href="mailto:hello@thepearlpetals.in" className="underline text-charcoal font-medium">hello@thepearlpetals.in</a>.
          </p>

// ==============================================================================
// FILE: src/app/track-order/page.tsx
// ==============================================================================

"use client";

import { useState, useEffect, Suspense } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { Order, OrderStatus } from "@/lib/types";
import { Compass, CheckCircle2, Clock, Truck, Package, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";

function TrackOrderContent() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const initialOrderNumber = searchParams.get("orderNumber") || "";

  const [orderNumber, setOrderNumber] = useState(initialOrderNumber);
  const [emailOrPhone, setEmailOrPhone] = useState("");
  const [order, setOrder] = useState<Order | null>(null);
  const [hasSearched, setHasSearched] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const handleSearch = async (e?: React.FormEvent) => {
    if (e) e.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setHasSearched(true);

    if (!orderNumber.trim()) {
      setError("Please provide an order number (e.g. PP-2026-1001).");
      return;
    }

    try {
      const res = await fetch(
        `/api/track-order?orderNumber=${encodeURIComponent(orderNumber.trim())}&contact=${encodeURIComponent(
          emailOrPhone.trim()
        )}`
      );
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok && data.order) {
        setOrder(data.order);
      } else {
        setOrder(null);
        setError("No matching order found. Please check your order number and contact detail.");
      }
    } catch {
      setError("Failed to fetch order tracking status.");
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (initialOrderNumber) {
      handleSearch();
    }
  }, [initialOrderNumber]);

  // Order status chronological progression
  const STEPS: { key: OrderStatus; label: string; desc: string }[] = [
    { key: "paid", label: "Payment Verified", desc: "Order confirmed & reserved" },
    { key: "processing", label: "Artisan Quality Check", desc: "Pieces inspected & prepped" },
    { key: "supplier_order_placed", label: "Supplier Allocation", desc: "Handcrafted batch reserved" },
    { key: "shipped", label: "Dispatched", desc: "Handed to BlueDart/Delhivery" },
    { key: "delivered", label: "Delivered", desc: "Safely received at address" },
  ];

  const getStepStatus = (stepKey: OrderStatus, currentStatus: OrderStatus) => {
    const orderIndex = STEPS.findIndex((s) => s.key === currentStatus);
    const thisIndex = STEPS.findIndex((s) => s.key === stepKey);

    if (currentStatus === "cancelled" || currentStatus === "refunded") {
      return "inactive";
    }
    if (thisIndex <= orderIndex) {
      return "completed";
    }
    if (thisIndex === orderIndex + 1) {
      return "current";
    }
    return "upcoming";
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-12 sm:py-20 space-y-12">
      
      {/* Editorial Header */}
      <div className="text-center space-y-3">
        <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-taupe font-sans block">
          Boutique Dispatch &amp; Tracking
        </span>
        <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl sm:text-4xl text-charcoal">
          Track Your Keepsake Parcel
        </h1>
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-charcoal/70 font-sans max-w-md mx-auto font-light">
          Enter your order number and email or phone number to check current dispatch progress and live courier status.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Lookup Form */}
      <form onSubmit={handleSearch} className="bg-white border border-[#EBE3D8] p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-4 max-w-xl mx-auto shadow-sm">
        <div className="space-y-1 text-xs font-sans">
          <label className="block text-charcoal font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">
            Order Number *
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            required
            value={orderNumber}
            onChange={(e) => setOrderNumber(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="e.g. PP-2026-1001"
            className="w-full bg-ivory/30 border border-champagne p-3 text-xs text-charcoal uppercase focus:outline-none focus:border-charcoal"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1 text-xs font-sans">
          <label className="block text-charcoal font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">
            Email or 10-Digit Mobile Number
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={emailOrPhone}
            onChange={(e) => setEmailOrPhone(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="e.g. customer@example.com or 9876543210"
            className="w-full bg-ivory/30 border border-champagne p-3 text-xs text-charcoal focus:outline-none focus:border-charcoal"
          />
        </div>

        <button
          type="submit"
          className="btn-boutique-primary w-full py-3.5 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
        >
          <Compass className="w-4 h-4 text-champagne" />
          <span>Track Parcel</span>
        </button>
      </form>

      {/* Error state */}
      {error && (
        <div className="max-w-xl mx-auto bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 p-4 text-xs font-sans text-rose-800 flex items-center gap-2">
          <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
          <span>{error}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Order Tracking Progress Card */}
      {order && (
        <div className="bg-white border border-[#EBE3D8] p-6 sm:p-10 space-y-8 shadow-sm">
          
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-[#EBE3D8] pb-6">
            <div>
              <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-taupe font-sans block">
                Order Reference
              </span>
              <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl text-charcoal font-medium">
                #{order.order_number}
              </h2>
              <span className="text-xs text-charcoal/70 font-sans mt-0.5 block">
                Placed on {new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleDateString("en-IN", { day: "numeric", month: "long", year: "numeric" })}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="text-left sm:text-right">
              <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-taupe font-sans block">Current Status</span>
              <span className="inline-block px-3 py-1 bg-ivory border border-champagne text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-medium text-charcoal capitalize mt-1">
                {order.status.replace("_", " ")}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Courier Dispatch Details */}
          {order.tracking_number && (
            <div className="bg-ivory/50 border border-champagne p-5 rounded-none flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 text-xs font-sans">
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <p className="font-medium text-charcoal flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Truck className="w-4 h-4 text-taupe-dark" />
                  Courier Partner: {order.courier_partner || "BlueDart Express"}
                </p>
                <p className="text-charcoal/70">
                  Tracking AWB: <span className="font-mono font-medium text-charcoal">{order.tracking_number}</span>
                </p>
              </div>

              {order.tracking_url && (
                <a
                  href={order.tracking_url}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="btn-boutique-primary py-2 px-4 text-[11px] shrink-0"
                >
                  Live Courier Tracking &rarr;
                </a>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Step Timeline */}
          <div className="space-y-6 pt-2">
            <h3 className="font-serif text-lg text-charcoal">Fulfillment Journey</h3>

            <div className="relative border-l-2 border-champagne ml-4 pl-6 space-y-8">
              {STEPS.map((step) => {
                const stepState = getStepStatus(step.key, order.status);
                const isCompleted = stepState === "completed";
                const isCurrent = stepState === "current";

                return (
                  <div key={step.key} className="relative">
                    {/* Step Icon Indicator */}
                    <div
                      className={`absolute -left-[33px] top-0 w-6 h-6 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-sans border-2 bg-white ${
                        isCompleted
                          ? "border-emerald-600 text-emerald-600"
                          : isCurrent
                          ? "border-charcoal text-charcoal ring-4 ring-ivory"
                          : "border-gray-300 text-gray-300"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {isCompleted ? (
                        <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                      ) : isCurrent ? (
                        <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-taupe-dark" />
                      ) : (
                        <Package className="w-3 h-3" />
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <h4
                        className={`font-serif text-base ${
                          isCompleted || isCurrent ? "text-charcoal font-medium" : "text-gray-400"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {step.label}
                      </h4>
                      <p className="text-xs text-charcoal/60 font-sans mt-0.5 font-light">
                        {step.desc}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Delivery Recipient Snapshot */}
          <div className="border-t border-[#EBE3D8] pt-6 flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between gap-6 text-xs font-sans">
            <div>
              <span className="text-taupe uppercase tracking-wider block font-medium">Destination:</span>
              <p className="text-charcoal font-medium">{order.customer.name}</p>
              <p className="text-charcoal/70">{order.customer.city}, {order.customer.state} ({order.customer.pincode})</p>
            </div>

            <div>
              <span className="text-taupe uppercase tracking-wider block font-medium">Parcel Contents:</span>
              <p className="text-charcoal font-medium">{order.items.length} {order.items.length === 1 ? "keepsake" : "keepsakes"}</p>
              <p className="text-charcoal/70">Total value: ₹{order.total_payable.toLocaleString("en-IN")}</p>
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>
      )}

      {/* Support Helper */}
      <div className="text-center text-xs text-charcoal/70 font-sans space-y-1">
        <p>Need assistance with your dispatch or address adjustments?</p>
        <p>
          Write to our boutique concierge at{" "}
          <a href="mailto:hello@thepearlpetals.in" className="text-charcoal underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-taupe">
            hello@thepearlpetals.in
          </a>
        </p>
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

export default function TrackOrderPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<div className="py-20 text-center text-xs font-sans text-taupe">Loading order tracker...</div>}>
      <TrackOrderContent />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

// ==============================================================================
// FILE: src/components/CartDrawer.tsx
// ==============================================================================

"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { X, Trash2, Plus, Minus, ShoppingBag, ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useCart } from "@/context/cart-context";

export default function CartDrawer() {
  const {
    items,
    isOpen,
    closeCart,
    removeItem,
    updateQuantity,
    subtotal,
    shippingFee,
    amountNeededForFreeShipping,
    freeShippingThreshold,
    totalPayable,
  } = useCart();

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const freeShippingProgress = Math.min(100, Math.round((subtotal / freeShippingThreshold) * 100));

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-hidden">
      {/* Backdrop */}
      <div
        className="absolute inset-0 bg-charcoal/40 backdrop-blur-sm transition-opacity"
        onClick={closeCart}
      />

      <div className="fixed inset-y-0 right-0 max-w-full flex pl-10">
        <div className="w-screen max-w-md bg-white border-l border-[#EBE3D8] shadow-2xl flex flex-col">
          
          {/* Header */}
          <div className="p-6 border-b border-[#EBE3D8] flex items-center justify-between bg-ivory/40">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
              <ShoppingBag className="w-5 h-5 text-taupe-dark" />
              <h2 className="font-serif text-xl tracking-wide text-charcoal">
                Your Shopping Bag ({items.reduce((s, i) => s + i.quantity, 0)})
              </h2>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={closeCart}
              className="p-1.5 text-charcoal/70 hover:text-charcoal transition-colors"
              aria-label="Close Shopping Bag"
            >
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Free Shipping Progress Indicator */}
          <div className="bg-ivory/60 px-6 py-3.5 border-b border-[#EBE3D8]">
            {amountNeededForFreeShipping > 0 ? (
              <p className="text-xs text-charcoal/80 font-sans">
                Add <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal">₹{amountNeededForFreeShipping}</span> more to unlock <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal">FREE Express Shipping</span>!
              </p>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-xs text-emerald-800 font-sans font-medium flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span>✨ You have unlocked Complimentary Express Shipping!</span>
              </p>
            )}
            <div className="w-full bg-[#E3D9CC] h-1.5 rounded-full mt-2 overflow-hidden">
              <div
                className="bg-taupe h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-500"
                style={{ width: `${freeShippingProgress}%` }}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Items List */}
          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-6">
            {items.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center py-12">
                <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-ivory flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
                  <ShoppingBag className="w-8 h-8 text-taupe" />
                </div>
                <h3 className="font-serif text-xl text-charcoal mb-1">Your bag is empty</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-charcoal/60 max-w-xs mb-6 font-sans">
                  Discover our delicate freshwater pearl jewelry and handmade accessories.
                </p>
                <Link
                  href="/shop"
                  onClick={closeCart}
                  className="btn-boutique-primary"
                >
                  Explore Collection
                </Link>
              </div>
            ) : (
              items.map((item) => {
                const img = item.product.images[0]?.image_url || "";
                const unitPrice = item.variant?.price_override ?? item.product.selling_price;
                const itemTotal = unitPrice * item.quantity;

                return (
                  <div
                    key={`${item.product.id}-${item.variant?.id || "default"}`}
                    className="flex gap-4 pb-6 border-b border-[#EBE3D8]/70"
                  >
                    <div className="relative w-20 h-24 bg-ivory/40 border border-[#EBE3D8] shrink-0 overflow-hidden">
                      {img && (
                        <Image
                          src={img}
                          alt={item.product.name}
                          fill
                          className="object-cover object-center"
                        />
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col justify-between">
                      <div>
                        <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                          <Link
                            href={`/product/${item.product.slug}`}
                            onClick={closeCart}
                            className="font-serif text-base text-charcoal hover:text-taupe transition-colors leading-snug line-clamp-1"
                          >
                            {item.product.name}
                          </Link>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => removeItem(item.product.id, item.variant?.id)}
                            className="text-taupe hover:text-rose-700 transition-colors p-1"
                            title="Remove item"
                          >
                            <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                          </button>
                        </div>

                        {item.variant && (
                          <span className="text-[11px] text-taupe block mt-0.5 font-sans">
                            {item.variant.title}
                          </span>
                        )}

                        <span className="text-xs font-medium text-charcoal block mt-1 font-sans">
                          ₹{unitPrice}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      {/* Quantity Controls */}
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-3">
                        <div className="flex items-center border border-champagne bg-white">
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() =>
                              updateQuantity(item.product.id, item.quantity - 1, item.variant?.id)
                            }
                            className="p-1.5 text-charcoal hover:bg-ivory transition-colors"
                            aria-label="Decrease quantity"
                          >
                            <Minus className="w-3 h-3" />
                          </button>
                          <span className="px-3 text-xs font-sans font-medium text-charcoal">
                            {item.quantity}
                          </span>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() =>
                              updateQuantity(item.product.id, item.quantity + 1, item.variant?.id)
                            }
                            className="p-1.5 text-charcoal hover:bg-ivory transition-colors"
                            aria-label="Increase quantity"
                          >
                            <Plus className="w-3 h-3" />
                          </button>
                        </div>

                        <span className="text-xs font-medium text-charcoal font-sans">
                          ₹{itemTotal.toLocaleString("en-IN")}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Footer & Checkout */}
          {items.length > 0 && (
            <div className="p-6 border-t border-[#EBE3D8] bg-ivory/30 space-y-4">
              <div className="space-y-2 text-xs font-sans">
                <div className="flex justify-between text-charcoal/80">
                  <span>Subtotal</span>
                  <span>₹{subtotal.toLocaleString("en-IN")}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-charcoal/80">
                  <span>Shipping (India)</span>
                  <span>{shippingFee === 0 ? "FREE" : `₹${shippingFee}`}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-sm font-medium text-charcoal pt-2 border-t border-[#EBE3D8]">
                  <span>Total Payable</span>
                  <span>₹{totalPayable.toLocaleString("en-IN")}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-2 pt-2">
                <Link
                  href="/checkout"
                  onClick={closeCart}
                  className="btn-boutique-primary w-full text-center flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-4"
                >
                  <span>Proceed to Prepaid Checkout</span>
                  <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                </Link>

                <Link
                  href="/cart"
                  onClick={closeCart}
                  className="btn-boutique-secondary w-full text-center py-2.5 text-[11px]"
                >
                  View Full Bag
                </Link>
              </div>

              <p className="text-[10px] text-center text-taupe font-sans">
                Prepaid orders only &bull; BlueDart / Delhivery insured dispatch
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


// ==============================================================================
// FILE: src/components/Footer.tsx
// ==============================================================================

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { ArrowRight, ShieldCheck, HeartHandshake, Sparkles, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";

export default function Footer() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<"idle" | "loading" | "success" | "error">("idle");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  const isAdminPage = pathname?.startsWith("/admin");
  if (isAdminPage) {
    return null;
  }

  const handleSubscribe = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!email || !email.includes("@")) return;

    setStatus("loading");
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/newsletter", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) {
        setStatus("success");
        setMessage(data.message || "Thank you for subscribing! Your 10% welcome code is WELCOME10.");
        setEmail("");
      } else {
        setStatus("error");
        setMessage(data.error || "Something went wrong. Please try again.");
      }
    } catch {
      setStatus("error");
      setMessage("Could not connect. Please try again.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <footer className="bg-ivory border-t border-champagne/40 pt-16 pb-12 text-charcoal">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        
        {/* Boutique Trust Pillars */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-8 pb-14 border-b border-champagne/40 text-center">
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-white flex items-center justify-center mb-3 shadow-sm border border-champagne/30">
              <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5 text-taupe-dark" />
            </div>
            <h4 className="font-serif text-lg font-medium">Conscious Craftsmanship</h4>
            <p className="text-xs text-charcoal/70 mt-1 max-w-xs font-sans">
              Hand-selected natural freshwater baroque pearls, hypoallergenic finishes, and artisanal quality.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-white flex items-center justify-center mb-3 shadow-sm border border-champagne/30">
              <HeartHandshake className="w-5 h-5 text-taupe-dark" />
            </div>
            <h4 className="font-serif text-lg font-medium">Boutique Unboxing</h4>
            <p className="text-xs text-charcoal/70 mt-1 max-w-xs font-sans">
              Every piece arrives in our signature keepsake jewelry box with custom tissue and satin ribbon.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-white flex items-center justify-center mb-3 shadow-sm border border-champagne/30">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5 text-taupe-dark" />
            </div>
            <h4 className="font-serif text-lg font-medium">Prepaid Express Care</h4>
            <p className="text-xs text-charcoal/70 mt-1 max-w-xs font-sans">
              Dedicated express dispatch across India with encrypted 256-bit bank and UPI payments.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Navigation Grid */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-10 py-14 border-b border-champagne/40">
          
          {/* Brand & Newsletter Column */}
          <div className="md:col-span-5 space-y-4">
            <span className="font-serif text-2xl tracking-wider text-charcoal block">
              Petal &amp; Pearl
            </span>
            <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-taupe font-sans -mt-2">
              Beauty in Every Little Detail
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-charcoal/80 leading-relaxed font-sans max-w-sm pt-2">
              An online sanctuary for fine accessories, baroque pearl keepsakes, and feminine daily adornments. Thoughtfully designed to make every morning ritual memorable.
            </p>

            {/* Newsletter Subscription */}
            <div className="pt-3">
              <h5 className="text-xs uppercase tracking-widest font-sans font-medium text-charcoal mb-2">
                Join The Petal Circle
              </h5>
              <p className="text-xs text-charcoal/70 mb-3 font-sans">
                Receive private boutique preview access and ₹100 off your first keepsake order.
              </p>
              
              <form onSubmit={handleSubscribe} className="flex max-w-sm">

                  type="email"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Enter your email address"
                  required
                  className="bg-white border border-champagne px-4 py-2.5 text-xs text-charcoal placeholder:text-taupe focus:outline-none focus:border-charcoal flex-1 font-sans rounded-none"

                  className="bg-charcoal text-ivory px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-sans uppercase tracking-widest hover:bg-taupe-dark transition-colors flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 disabled:opacity-50"

                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { ShoppingBag, Search, Menu, X, Compass } from "lucide-react";
import { useCart } from "@/context/cart-context";

export default function Navbar() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const { openCart, itemCount } = useCart();
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  // If inside /admin, suppress main customer navbar or provide simple link
  const isAdminPage = pathname?.startsWith("/admin");
  if (isAdminPage) {
    return null;
  }

  const navLinks = [
    { label: "Shop All", href: "/shop" },
    { label: "🔥 Hottest Picks", href: "/shop?filter=hottest" },
    { label: "Hair Clips", href: "/category/hair-accessories" },
    { label: "Jewellery", href: "/category/jewellery" },
    { label: "Everyday Essentials", href: "/category/everyday-essentials" },
    { label: "Gifts", href: "/category/gifts" },
  ];

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-[#EBE3D8] transition-all">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between h-20">
          
          {/* Mobile hamburger button */}
          <div className="flex items-center lg:hidden">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(!mobileMenuOpen)}
              className="p-2 text-charcoal hover:text-taupe focus:outline-none"
              aria-label="Toggle Navigation Menu"
            >
              {mobileMenuOpen ? <X className="w-6 h-6" /> : <Menu className="w-6 h-6" />}
            </button>
            <Link href="/search" className="p-2 text-charcoal hover:text-taupe ml-1" aria-label="Search Store">
              <Search className="w-5 h-5" />
            </Link>
          </div>

          {/* Desktop Left Nav */}
          <nav className="hidden lg:flex items-center space-x-8">
            {navLinks.slice(0, 3).map((link) => {
              const active = pathname === link.href;
              return (
                <Link
                  key={link.href}
                  href={link.href}
                  className={`text-xs uppercase tracking-widest font-sans transition-colors py-1 relative ${
                    active ? "text-charcoal font-medium" : "text-charcoal/70 hover:text-charcoal"
                  }`}
                >
                  {link.label}
                  {active && (
                    <span className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-full h-[1.5px] bg-champagne" />
                  )}
                </Link>
              );
            })}
          </nav>

          {/* Center Brand Identity */}
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center">
            <Link href="/" className="group">
              <span className="font-serif text-2xl sm:text-3xl tracking-wider text-charcoal block transition-transform group-hover:scale-[1.01]">
                Petal &amp; Pearl
              </span>
              <span className="text-[9px] uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-taupe block font-sans -mt-1 font-normal">
                Beauty in Every Little Detail
              </span>
            </Link>
          </div>

          {/* Desktop Right Nav & Actions */}
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-6">
            <nav className="hidden lg:flex items-center space-x-8">
              {navLinks.slice(3).map((link) => {
                const active = pathname === link.href;
                return (
                  <Link
                    key={link.href}
                    href={link.href}
                    className={`text-xs uppercase tracking-widest font-sans transition-colors py-1 relative ${
                      active ? "text-charcoal font-medium" : "text-charcoal/70 hover:text-charcoal"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {link.label}
                    {active && (
                      <span className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-full h-[1.5px] bg-champagne" />
                    )}
                  </Link>
                );
              })}
            </nav>

            <div className="flex items-center space-x-4 border-l border-[#EBE3D8] pl-6">
              <Link
                href="/search"
                className="hidden sm:inline-flex p-1.5 text-charcoal/80 hover:text-charcoal transition-colors"

                <Search className="w-5 h-5" />
              </Link>

              <Link
                href="/track-order"
                className="hidden sm:inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs uppercase tracking-widest font-sans text-charcoal/70 hover:text-charcoal transition-colors"

              </Link>

              {/* Shopping Bag Button */}
              <button

      </div>

      {/* Mobile Menu Dropdown */}
      {mobileMenuOpen && (

          <div className="flex flex-col space-y-4">
            {navLinks.map((link) => (
              <Link

          <div className="pt-4 border-t border-[#EBE3D8] flex flex-col space-y-3 text-xs uppercase tracking-widest font-sans text-taupe-dark">
            <Link
              href="/track-order"
              onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 hover:text-charcoal"
            >
              <Compass className="w-4 h-4" />

            </Link>

              href="/about"
              onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
              className="hover:text-charcoal"
            >
              About Petal &amp; Pearl
            </Link>
            <Link
              href="/contact"
              onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
              className="hover:text-charcoal"
            >
              Customer Care &amp; Inquiries
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </header>
  );
}

// FILE: src/components/ProductCard.tsx
