"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { PublicProduct } from "@/lib/types";
import { useCart } from "@/context/cart-context";

interface ProductCardProps {
  product: PublicProduct;
  priority?: boolean;
}

export default function ProductCard({ product, priority = false }: ProductCardProps) {
  const { addItem } = useCart();
  const primaryImage = product.images.find((img) => img.is_primary) || product.images[0];
  const secondaryImage = product.images.length > 1 ? product.images[1] : null;

  return (
    <div className="group flex flex-col h-full bg-white border border-[#EBE3D8] transition-all duration-300 hover:border-champagne hover:shadow-[0_8px_30px_rgb(216,197,171,0.15)]">
      
      {/* Image Container with hover swap */}
      <Link href={`/product/${product.slug}`} className="relative block aspect-[4/5] overflow-hidden bg-ivory/50">
        {primaryImage && (
          <Image
            src={primaryImage.image_url}
            alt={primaryImage.alt_text || product.name}
            fill
            sizes="(max-width: 640px) 50vw, (max-width: 1024px) 33vw, 25vw"
            priority={priority}
            unoptimized
            referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
            onError={(e) => {
              const target = e.target as HTMLImageElement;
              target.srcset = "";
              target.src = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1535632066927-ab7c9ab60908?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80";
            }}
            className={`object-cover object-center transition-all duration-700 group-hover:scale-105 ${
              secondaryImage ? "group-hover:opacity-0" : ""
            }`}
          />
        )}

        {secondaryImage && (
          <Image
            src={secondaryImage.image_url}
            alt={secondaryImage.alt_text || product.name}
            fill
            sizes="(max-width: 640px) 50vw, (max-width: 1024px) 33vw, 25vw"
            unoptimized
            referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
            className="object-cover object-center absolute inset-0 opacity-0 transition-all duration-700 group-hover:opacity-100 group-hover:scale-105"
          />
        )}

        {/* Badges */}
        <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 flex flex-col gap-1.5 z-10">
          {product.is_bestseller && (
            <span className="bg-[#2D2A26] text-[#FAF8F5] text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wider px-2.5 py-1 font-sans font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 shadow-sm">
              <span>🔥 Hottest Pick</span>
            </span>
          )}
          {product.is_new && !product.is_bestseller && (
            <span className="bg-ivory text-charcoal text-[9px] uppercase tracking-widest px-2.5 py-1 font-sans font-medium border border-champagne">
              ✦ New Drop
            </span>
          )}
          {product.stock <= 5 && product.stock > 0 && (
            <span className="bg-amber-50 text-amber-900 border border-amber-200 text-[9px] uppercase tracking-widest px-2 py-0.5 font-sans">
              Only {product.stock} left
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Quick Add Overlay on desktop hover */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopPropagation();
            addItem(product, product.variants[0], 1);
          }}
          className="absolute bottom-3 inset-x-3 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-sm text-charcoal text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest py-2.5 px-4 font-sans font-medium shadow-sm border border-champagne opacity-0 translate-y-2 group-hover:opacity-100 group-hover:translate-y-0 transition-all duration-300 hidden sm:flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 hover:bg-charcoal hover:text-white"
          aria-label={`Quick add ${product.name} to shopping bag`}
        >
          <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          Quick Add
        </button>
      </Link>

      {/* Product Information */}
      <div className="p-4 flex flex-col flex-1 justify-between bg-white">
        <div>
          <span className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-taupe font-sans block mb-1">
            {product.subcategory === "claw-clips"
              ? "Claw Clip"
              : product.subcategory === "hair-bows"
              ? "Hair Bow"
              : product.subcategory.replace("-", " ")}
          </span>
          <Link href={`/product/${product.slug}`} className="block">
            <h3 className="font-serif text-base sm:text-lg text-charcoal leading-snug line-clamp-1 hover:text-taupe transition-colors">
              {product.name}
            </h3>
          </Link>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-[#EBE3D8]/60 flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="font-sans text-sm font-medium text-charcoal tracking-wide">
            ₹{product.selling_price.toLocaleString("en-IN")}
          </span>

          {/* Mobile quick add button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => addItem(product, product.variants[0], 1)}
            className="sm:hidden p-1.5 text-charcoal hover:text-taupe border border-champagne rounded-none"
            aria-label={`Add ${product.name} to bag`}
          >
            <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}


// ==============================================================================
// FILE: src/context/cart-context.tsx
// ==============================================================================

"use client";
// ==============================================================================
// PETAL & PEARL — SHOPPING CART CONTEXT
// Domain: https://thepearlpetals.in
// State management, localStorage persistence & live pricing
// ==============================================================================

import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { PublicProduct, ProductVariant, CartItem } from "../lib/types";
import { calculateShippingFee, DEFAULT_PRICING_SETTINGS } from "../lib/pricing-engine";

interface CartContextType {
  items: CartItem[];
  isOpen: boolean;
  openCart: () => void;
  closeCart: () => void;
  addItem: (product: PublicProduct, variant?: ProductVariant, quantity?: number) => void;
  removeItem: (productId: string, variantId?: string) => void;
  updateQuantity: (productId: string, quantity: number, variantId?: string) => void;
  clearCart: () => void;
  itemCount: number;
  subtotal: number;
  shippingFee: number;
  freeShippingThreshold: number;
  amountNeededForFreeShipping: number;
  totalPayable: number;
}

const CartContext = createContext<CartContextType | undefined>(undefined);

const CART_STORAGE_KEY = "petal_pearl_cart_v1";

export function CartProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<CartItem[]>([]);
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isLoaded, setIsLoaded] = useState(false);

  // Load from localStorage on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(CART_STORAGE_KEY);
      if (saved) {
        setItems(JSON.parse(saved));
      }
    } catch {
      // Fallback
    } finally {
      setIsLoaded(true);
    }
  }, []);

  // Save to localStorage on change
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isLoaded) return;
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(CART_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(items));
    } catch {
      // Fallback
    }
  }, [items, isLoaded]);

  const openCart = () => setIsOpen(true);
  const closeCart = () => setIsOpen(false);

  const addItem = (product: PublicProduct, variant?: ProductVariant, quantity = 1) => {
    setItems((prev) => {
      const existingIdx = prev.findIndex(
        (i) => i.product.id === product.id && (variant ? i.variant?.id === variant.id : !i.variant)
      );

      if (existingIdx !== -1) {
        const next = [...prev];
        const newQty = next[existingIdx].quantity + quantity;
        // Check stock boundary
        const maxStock = variant ? variant.stock : product.stock;
        next[existingIdx].quantity = Math.min(newQty, maxStock);
        return next;
      }

      return [...prev, { product, variant, quantity }];
    });
    setIsOpen(true);
  };

  const removeItem = (productId: string, variantId?: string) => {
    setItems((prev) =>
      prev.filter(
        (i) => !(i.product.id === productId && (variantId ? i.variant?.id === variantId : !i.variant))
      )
    );
  };

  const updateQuantity = (productId: string, quantity: number, variantId?: string) => {
    if (quantity <= 0) {
      removeItem(productId, variantId);
      return;
    }

    setItems((prev) =>
      prev.map((i) => {
        if (i.product.id === productId && (variantId ? i.variant?.id === variantId : !i.variant)) {
          const maxStock = i.variant ? i.variant.stock : i.product.stock;
          return { ...i, quantity: Math.min(quantity, maxStock) };
        }
        return i;
      })
    );
  };

  const clearCart = () => {
    setItems([]);
  };

  const itemCount = items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0);

  const subtotal = items.reduce((sum, item) => {
    const unitPrice = item.variant?.price_override ?? item.product.selling_price;
    return sum + unitPrice * item.quantity;
  }, 0);

  const shippingFee = calculateShippingFee(subtotal, DEFAULT_PRICING_SETTINGS);
  const freeShippingThreshold = DEFAULT_PRICING_SETTINGS.freeShippingThreshold;
  const amountNeededForFreeShipping = Math.max(0, freeShippingThreshold - subtotal);
  const totalPayable = subtotal + shippingFee;

  return (
    <CartContext.Provider
      value={{
        items,
        isOpen,
        openCart,
        closeCart,
        addItem,
        removeItem,
        updateQuantity,
        clearCart,
        itemCount,
        subtotal,
        shippingFee,
        freeShippingThreshold,
        amountNeededForFreeShipping,
        totalPayable,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </CartContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useCart() {
  const context = useContext(CartContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useCart must be used within a CartProvider");
  }
  return context;
}

// ==============================================================================
// FILE: src/lib/pricing-engine.ts
// ==============================================================================

// ==============================================================================
// PETAL & PEARL — PRICING & CONTRIBUTION ENGINE
// Domain: https://thepearlpetals.in
// Rule: Selling Price = Supplier Cost + Markup (Default ₹100)
// ==============================================================================

export interface PricingSettings {
  defaultMarkup: number; // default ₹100
  freeShippingThreshold: number; // default ₹999
  flatShippingFee: number; // default ₹99
  paymentGatewayFeePercent: number; // default 2% for Razorpay
  paymentGatewayGstPercent: number; // default 18% on gateway fee
}

export const DEFAULT_PRICING_SETTINGS: PricingSettings = {
  defaultMarkup: 100,
  freeShippingThreshold: 999,
  flatShippingFee: 99,
  paymentGatewayFeePercent: 0.02, // 2%
  paymentGatewayGstPercent: 0.18, // 18%
};

/**
 * Calculates the consumer-facing selling price from the supplier cost.
 * Selling price = supplierCost + markup
 *
 * @param supplierCost The verified unit cost charged by the authorized supplier
 * @param customMarkup Optional specific markup; defaults to DEFAULT_PRICING_SETTINGS.defaultMarkup (₹100)
 * @returns sellingPrice (number)
 */
export function calculateSellingPrice(supplierCost: number, customMarkup?: number | null): number {
  const markup = customMarkup !== undefined && customMarkup !== null ? customMarkup : DEFAULT_PRICING_SETTINGS.defaultMarkup;
  const cost = Math.max(0, Number(supplierCost) || 0);
  return Math.round(cost + markup);
}

/**
 * Calculates shipping fee based on subtotal.
 * Orders >= freeShippingThreshold (₹999) receive FREE shipping.
 * Otherwise, flat shipping fee (₹99) applies.
 */
export function calculateShippingFee(subtotal: number, settings: PricingSettings = DEFAULT_PRICING_SETTINGS): number {
  if (subtotal <= 0) return 0;
  return subtotal >= settings.freeShippingThreshold ? 0 : settings.flatShippingFee;
}

/**
 * Strips confidential supplier cost and internal margins before sending product data to client.
 */
export function sanitizePublicProduct<T extends { supplier_cost?: number; custom_markup?: number | null }>(product: T): Omit<T, "supplier_cost" | "custom_markup"> {
  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars
  const { supplier_cost, custom_markup, ...publicData } = product;
  return publicData;
}

export interface ContributionBreakdown {
  sellingPrice: number;
  supplierCost: number;
  grossMargin: number;
  shippingCustomerPaid: number;
  shippingActualCost: number;
  paymentGatewayFee: number;
  packagingCost: number;
  netContribution: number;
  contributionMarginPercent: number;
}

/**
 * Computes actual contribution after all fulfillment and operational deductions.
 * Formula: Net Contribution = Selling Price + Shipping Paid - Supplier Cost - Actual Shipping - Gateway Fee - Packaging
 */
export function calculateContribution(params: {
  sellingPrice: number;
  supplierCost: number;
  shippingCustomerPaid: number;
  shippingActualCost?: number;
  packagingCost?: number;
  gatewayFeePercent?: number;
}): ContributionBreakdown {
  const {
    sellingPrice,
    supplierCost,
    shippingCustomerPaid,
    shippingActualCost = 65, // Standard Delhivery/BlueDart e-commerce rate ~₹65
    packagingCost = 25, // Luxury branded box + butter paper + ribbon ~₹25
    gatewayFeePercent = 0.0236, // 2% Razorpay + 18% GST = 2.36%
  } = params;

  const totalCollected = sellingPrice + shippingCustomerPaid;
  const paymentGatewayFee = Math.round(totalCollected * gatewayFeePercent * 100) / 100;
  const grossMargin = sellingPrice - supplierCost;
  
  const totalExpenses = supplierCost + shippingActualCost + paymentGatewayFee + packagingCost;
  const netContribution = Math.round((totalCollected - totalExpenses) * 100) / 100;
  const contributionMarginPercent = totalCollected > 0 
    ? Math.round((netContribution / totalCollected) * 10000) / 100 
    : 0;

  return {
    sellingPrice,
    supplierCost,
    grossMargin,
    shippingCustomerPaid,
    shippingActualCost,
    paymentGatewayFee,
    packagingCost,
    netContribution,
    contributionMarginPercent,
  };
}


// ==============================================================================
// FILE: src/lib/public-catalog.ts
// ==============================================================================

// ==============================================================================
// PETAL & PEARL — CLIENT-SAFE PUBLIC CATALOGUE
// Pure browser-compatible data layer with NO Node.js (fs) dependencies
// ==============================================================================

import { PublicProduct } from "./types";
import { SEED_PRODUCTS, CATEGORIES_DATA } from "../data/seed-data";
import { sanitizePublicProduct } from "./pricing-engine";

export { CATEGORIES_DATA };

// Base public catalogue (Supplier costs strictly sanitized)
const PUBLIC_CATALOGUE: PublicProduct[] = SEED_PRODUCTS.filter((p) => p.is_visible).map(
  sanitizePublicProduct
);

export function getPublicProducts(filters?: {
  category?: string;
  subcategory?: string;
  featured?: boolean;
  bestseller?: boolean;
  search?: string;
}): PublicProduct[] {
  let list = [...PUBLIC_CATALOGUE];

  if (filters?.category) {
    list = list.filter((p) => p.category === filters.category);
  }
  if (filters?.subcategory) {
    list = list.filter((p) => p.subcategory === filters.subcategory);
  }
  if (filters?.featured) {
    list = list.filter((p) => p.is_featured);
  }
  if (filters?.bestseller) {
    list = list.filter((p) => p.is_bestseller);
  }
  if (filters?.search) {
    const q = filters.search.toLowerCase().trim();
    list = list.filter(
      (p) =>
        p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        p.description.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        p.tags.some((t) => t.toLowerCase().includes(q))
    );
  }

  return list;
}

export function getPublicProductBySlug(slug: string): PublicProduct | null {
  const p = PUBLIC_CATALOGUE.find((item) => item.slug === slug);
  return p || null;
}

export function getPublicProductById(id: string): PublicProduct | null {
  const p = PUBLIC_CATALOGUE.find((item) => item.id === id);
  return p || null;
}


// ==============================================================================
// FILE: src/lib/store.ts
// ==============================================================================

// ==============================================================================
// PETAL & PEARL — CENTRAL DATA STORE & REPOSITORY
// Domain: https://thepearlpetals.in
// Handles dynamic CRUD, public sanitization, order tracking & admin metrics
// ==============================================================================

import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { Product, PublicProduct, Order, Supplier, OrderStatus } from "./types";
import { SEED_PRODUCTS, SEED_SUPPLIERS } from "../data/seed-data";
import { calculateSellingPrice, sanitizePublicProduct, DEFAULT_PRICING_SETTINGS, PricingSettings } from "./pricing-engine";

const DATA_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), "data");
const PRODUCTS_FILE = path.join(DATA_DIR, "products.json");
const ORDERS_FILE = path.join(DATA_DIR, "orders.json");
const SUPPLIERS_FILE = path.join(DATA_DIR, "suppliers.json");
const SETTINGS_FILE = path.join(DATA_DIR, "settings.json");
const SUBSCRIBERS_FILE = path.join(DATA_DIR, "subscribers.json");

function ensureDirectoryExists() {
  if (!fs.existsSync(DATA_DIR)) {
    try {
      fs.mkdirSync(DATA_DIR, { recursive: true });
    } catch {
      // fallback in restricted env
    }
  }
}

// In-memory cache with fallback to disk persistence
let memoryProducts: Product[] = [...SEED_PRODUCTS];
let memorySuppliers: Supplier[] = [...SEED_SUPPLIERS];
let memoryOrders: Order[] = [];
let memorySettings: PricingSettings = { ...DEFAULT_PRICING_SETTINGS };
let memorySubscribers: Array<{ email: string; created_at: string; code: string }> = [];


// Helper to save to disk safely
function saveToFile(filePath: string, data: unknown) {
  try {
    ensureDirectoryExists();
    fs.writeFileSync(filePath, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2), "utf8");
  } catch {
    // If running in readonly context, memory state suffices
  }
}

// Initialize from file if exists
function loadInitialState() {
  try {
    if (fs.existsSync(PRODUCTS_FILE)) {
      memoryProducts = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(PRODUCTS_FILE, "utf8"));
    }
    if (fs.existsSync(ORDERS_FILE)) {
      memoryOrders = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(ORDERS_FILE, "utf8"));
    }
    if (fs.existsSync(SUPPLIERS_FILE)) {
      memorySuppliers = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(SUPPLIERS_FILE, "utf8"));
    }
    if (fs.existsSync(SETTINGS_FILE)) {
      memorySettings = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(SETTINGS_FILE, "utf8"));
    }
    if (fs.existsSync(SUBSCRIBERS_FILE)) {
      memorySubscribers = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(SUBSCRIBERS_FILE, "utf8"));
    }
  } catch {
    // continue with memory defaults
  }
}

loadInitialState();

// ==============================================================================
// PUBLIC FACING DATA ACCESS (SUPPLIER COSTS STRIPPED)
// ==============================================================================

export function getPublicProducts(filters?: {
  category?: string;
  subcategory?: string;
  featured?: boolean;
  bestseller?: boolean;
  search?: string;
}): PublicProduct[] {
  let list = memoryProducts.filter((p) => p.is_visible);

  if (filters?.category) {
    list = list.filter((p) => p.category === filters.category);
  }
  if (filters?.subcategory) {
    list = list.filter((p) => p.subcategory === filters.subcategory);
  }
  if (filters?.featured) {
    list = list.filter((p) => p.is_featured);
  }
  if (filters?.bestseller) {
    list = list.filter((p) => p.is_bestseller);
  }
  if (filters?.search) {
    const q = filters.search.toLowerCase();
    list = list.filter(
      (p) =>
        p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        p.description.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        p.tags.some((t) => t.toLowerCase().includes(q))
    );
  }

  return list.map(sanitizePublicProduct);
}

export function getPublicProductBySlug(slug: string): PublicProduct | null {
  const p = memoryProducts.find((item) => item.slug === slug && item.is_visible);
  if (!p) return null;
  return sanitizePublicProduct(p);
}

export function getPublicProductById(id: string): PublicProduct | null {
  const p = memoryProducts.find((item) => item.id === id && item.is_visible);
  if (!p) return null;
  return sanitizePublicProduct(p);
}

// ==============================================================================
// ORDER & TRACKING REPOSITORY
// ==============================================================================

export function createOrder(orderData: Omit<Order, "id" | "order_number" | "created_at" | "updated_at">): Order {
  const count = memoryOrders.length + 1001;
  const orderNumber = `PP-2026-${count}`;
  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  const newOrder: Order = {
    ...orderData,
    id: `ord-${Date.now()}`,
    order_number: orderNumber,
    created_at: now,
    updated_at: now,
  };

  memoryOrders.unshift(newOrder);
  saveToFile(ORDERS_FILE, memoryOrders);
  return newOrder;
}

export function getOrderByNumber(orderNumber: string): Order | null {
  return memoryOrders.find((o) => o.order_number.toUpperCase() === orderNumber.trim().toUpperCase()) || null;
}

export function trackOrder(orderNumber: string, emailOrPhone: string): Order | null {
  const cleanNum = orderNumber.trim().toUpperCase();
  const cleanContact = emailOrPhone.trim().toLowerCase();

  const order = memoryOrders.find(
    (o) =>
      o.order_number.toUpperCase() === cleanNum &&
      (o.customer.email.toLowerCase() === cleanContact ||
        o.customer.phone.replace(/\s+/g, "").includes(cleanContact.replace(/\s+/g, "")))
  );

  return order || null;
}

// ==============================================================================
// ADMIN STORE REPOSITORY (FULL ACCESS)
// ==============================================================================

export function getAllProductsAdmin(): Product[] {
  return memoryProducts;
}

export function getProductByIdAdmin(id: string): Product | null {
  return memoryProducts.find((p) => p.id === id) || null;
}

export function saveProductAdmin(productData: Partial<Product> & { name: string; category: Product["category"]; subcategory: Product["subcategory"]; supplier_cost: number }): Product {
  const isUpdate = Boolean(productData.id);
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const markup = productData.custom_markup !== undefined ? productData.custom_markup : memorySettings.defaultMarkup;
  const sellingPrice = calculateSellingPrice(productData.supplier_cost, markup);

  if (isUpdate && productData.id) {
    const idx = memoryProducts.findIndex((p) => p.id === productData.id);
    if (idx !== -1) {
      memoryProducts[idx] = {
        ...memoryProducts[idx],
        ...productData,
        selling_price: sellingPrice,
        updated_at: now,
      };
      saveToFile(PRODUCTS_FILE, memoryProducts);
      return memoryProducts[idx];
    }
  }

  // Create new
  const id = `prod-${Date.now()}`;
  const slug = productData.slug || productData.name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/(^-|-$)/g, "");
  const sku = productData.sku || `PP-${productData.category.substring(0, 2).toUpperCase()}-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`;

  const newProduct: Product = {
    id,
    sku,
    name: productData.name,
    slug,
    category: productData.category,
    subcategory: productData.subcategory,
    description: productData.description || "",
    supplier_cost: Number(productData.supplier_cost),
    selling_price: sellingPrice,
    custom_markup: productData.custom_markup,
    stock: productData.stock || 0,
    is_featured: productData.is_featured || false,
    is_bestseller: productData.is_bestseller || false,
    is_new: productData.is_new !== undefined ? productData.is_new : true,
    is_visible: productData.is_visible !== undefined ? productData.is_visible : true,
    tags: productData.tags || [],
    product_link: productData.product_link || productData.source_url,
    source_url: productData.source_url || productData.product_link,
    images: productData.images || [
      {
        id: `img-${Date.now()}`,
        image_url: "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1535632066927-ab7c9ab60908?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80",
        alt_text: productData.name,
        display_order: 1,
        is_primary: true,
      },
    ],
    variants: productData.variants || [
      { id: `var-${Date.now()}`, title: "Default Style", sku: `${sku}-DEF`, stock: productData.stock || 0, attributes: {} },
    ],
    created_at: now,
    updated_at: now,
  };

  memoryProducts.unshift(newProduct);
  saveToFile(PRODUCTS_FILE, memoryProducts);
  return newProduct;
}

export function updateDefaultMarkupAdmin(newMarkup: number): { updatedCount: number; newMarkup: number } {
  memorySettings.defaultMarkup = Number(newMarkup);
  saveToFile(SETTINGS_FILE, memorySettings);

  // Recalculate selling price for all products that don't have a custom override
  let updatedCount = 0;
  memoryProducts = memoryProducts.map((p) => {
    if (p.custom_markup === null || p.custom_markup === undefined) {
      updatedCount++;
      return {
        ...p,
        selling_price: calculateSellingPrice(p.supplier_cost, memorySettings.defaultMarkup),
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
    }
    return p;
  });

  saveToFile(PRODUCTS_FILE, memoryProducts);
  return { updatedCount, newMarkup: memorySettings.defaultMarkup };
}

export function deleteProductAdmin(id: string): boolean {
  const initLen = memoryProducts.length;
  memoryProducts = memoryProducts.filter((p) => p.id !== id);
  if (memoryProducts.length !== initLen) {
    saveToFile(PRODUCTS_FILE, memoryProducts);
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

export function getAllOrdersAdmin(): Order[] {
  return memoryOrders;
}

export function updateOrderStatusAdmin(params: {
  orderNumber: string;
  status: OrderStatus;
  supplierOrderRef?: string;
  trackingNumber?: string;
  courierPartner?: string;
  actualShippingCost?: number;
}): Order | null {
  const idx = memoryOrders.findIndex((o) => o.order_number === params.orderNumber);
  if (idx === -1) return null;

  const current = memoryOrders[idx];
  const updated: Order = {
    ...current,
    status: params.status,
    supplier_order_ref: params.supplierOrderRef !== undefined ? params.supplierOrderRef : current.supplier_order_ref,
    tracking_number: params.trackingNumber !== undefined ? params.trackingNumber : current.tracking_number,
    courier_partner: params.courierPartner !== undefined ? params.courierPartner : current.courier_partner,
    actual_shipping_cost: params.actualShippingCost !== undefined ? params.actualShippingCost : current.actual_shipping_cost,
    tracking_url: params.trackingNumber ? `https://track.thepearlpetals.in/${params.trackingNumber}` : current.tracking_url,
    updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  };

  memoryOrders[idx] = updated;
  saveToFile(ORDERS_FILE, memoryOrders);
  return updated;
}

export function getAllSuppliersAdmin(): Supplier[] {
  return memorySuppliers;
}

export function saveSupplierAdmin(supplier: Omit<Supplier, "id" | "created_at"> & { id?: string }): Supplier {
  const isUpdate = Boolean(supplier.id);
  if (isUpdate && supplier.id) {
    const idx = memorySuppliers.findIndex((s) => s.id === supplier.id);
    if (idx !== -1) {
      memorySuppliers[idx] = {
        ...memorySuppliers[idx],
        ...supplier,
      };
      saveToFile(SUPPLIERS_FILE, memorySuppliers);
      return memorySuppliers[idx];
    }
  }

  const newSupplier: Supplier = {
    ...supplier,
    id: `sup-${Date.now()}`,
    created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  };

  memorySuppliers.push(newSupplier);
  saveToFile(SUPPLIERS_FILE, memorySuppliers);
  return newSupplier;
}

export function addSubscriber(email: string): { success: boolean; code: string; message: string } {
  const cleanEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
  const exists = memorySubscribers.find((s) => s.email === cleanEmail);
  if (exists) {
    return { success: true, code: exists.code, message: "Welcome back! Your 10% welcome code is active." };
  }

  const code = "WELCOME10";
  memorySubscribers.push({
    email: cleanEmail,
    created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    code,
  });
  saveToFile(SUBSCRIBERS_FILE, memorySubscribers);
  return { success: true, code, message: "Welcome to Petal & Pearl! Use code WELCOME10 for 10% off." };
}

export function getSubscribersAdmin() {
  return memorySubscribers;
}

export function getStoreSettings(): PricingSettings {
  return memorySettings;
}

export function getFinancialOverviewAdmin() {
  const totalOrders = memoryOrders.length;
  const paidOrders = memoryOrders.filter((o) => o.payment_status === "paid" || o.status !== "pending_payment");
  
  const totalRevenue = paidOrders.reduce((acc, o) => acc + o.total_payable, 0);
  const totalSupplierCost = paidOrders.reduce((acc, o) => acc + o.total_supplier_cost, 0);
  const totalCustomerShippingPaid = paidOrders.reduce((acc, o) => acc + o.shipping_fee, 0);
  
  // Expenses calculation
  const totalActualShippingExpenses = paidOrders.reduce((acc, o) => acc + (o.actual_shipping_cost || 65), 0);
  const totalGatewayCharges = Math.round(totalRevenue * 0.0236 * 100) / 100; // 2.36% (2% + 18% GST)
  const packagingExpenses = paidOrders.length * 25; // ₹25 per luxury boutique box
  
  const netContribution = Math.round(
    (totalRevenue - totalSupplierCost - totalActualShippingExpenses - totalGatewayCharges - packagingExpenses) * 100
  ) / 100;

  const contributionMarginPercent = totalRevenue > 0 
    ? Math.round((netContribution / totalRevenue) * 10000) / 100 
    : 0;

  return {
    totalOrders,
    paidOrdersCount: paidOrders.length,
    totalRevenue,
    totalSupplierCost,
    totalCustomerShippingPaid,
    totalActualShippingExpenses,
    totalGatewayCharges,
    packagingExpenses,
    netContribution,
    contributionMarginPercent,
    currentDefaultMarkup: memorySettings.defaultMarkup,
  };
}


// ==============================================================================
// FILE: src/lib/types.ts
// ==============================================================================

// ==============================================================================
// PETAL & PEARL — CORE APPLICATION TYPES
// ==============================================================================

export type ProductCategory = 
  | "jewellery" 
  | "hair-accessories" 
  | "everyday-essentials" 
  | "gifts";

export type ProductSubcategory = 
  | "earrings" 
  | "necklaces" 
  | "bracelets" 
  | "rings" 
  | "claw-clips" 
  | "scrunchies" 
  | "hair-bows" 
  | "phone-charms" 
  | "pouches" 
  | "gift-sets";

export interface ProductVariant {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  sku: string;
  price_override?: number;
  stock: number;
  attributes: Record<string, string>; // e.g. { color: "Champagne Gold", finish: "High Polish" }
}

export interface ProductImage {
  id: string;
  image_url: string;
  alt_text: string;
  display_order: number;
  is_primary?: boolean;
}

export interface Product {
  id: string;
  sku: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  category: ProductCategory;
  subcategory: ProductSubcategory;
  description: string;
  supplier_cost: number; // Confidential - excluded from public view
  selling_price: number;
  custom_markup?: number | null;
  stock: number;
  is_featured: boolean;
  is_bestseller: boolean;
  is_new: boolean;
  is_visible: boolean;
  images: ProductImage[];
  variants: ProductVariant[];
  tags: string[];
  product_link?: string;
  source_url?: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

export type PublicProduct = Omit<Product, "supplier_cost" | "custom_markup">;

export type OrderStatus =
  | "pending_payment"
  | "paid"
  | "processing"
  | "supplier_order_placed"
  | "shipped"
  | "delivered"
  | "cancelled"
  | "refund_pending"
  | "refunded";

export type PaymentStatus = "unpaid" | "paid" | "failed" | "refunded";

export interface OrderItem {
  id: string;
  product_id: string;
  variant_id?: string;
  product_name: string;
  variant_title?: string;
  sku: string;
  unit_selling_price: number;
  unit_supplier_cost: number;
  quantity: number;
  total_price: number;
  total_cost: number;
  image_url?: string;
}

export interface CustomerAddress {
  name: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  house: string;
  street: string;
  city: string;
  state: string;
  pincode: string;
  country: "India";
}

export interface Order {
  id: string;
  order_number: string;
  user_id?: string;
  customer: CustomerAddress;
  items: OrderItem[];
  status: OrderStatus;
  payment_status: PaymentStatus;
  subtotal: number;
  shipping_fee: number;
  discount_amount: number;
  total_payable: number;
  total_supplier_cost: number;
  total_contribution: number;
  supplier_order_ref?: string;
  courier_partner?: string;
  tracking_number?: string;
  tracking_url?: string;
  actual_shipping_cost?: number;
  refund_status?: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

export interface CartItem {
  product: PublicProduct;
  variant?: ProductVariant;
  quantity: number;
}

export interface Supplier {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  contact_email: string;
  contact_phone: string;
  website_url: string;
  resale_permission_status: "verified_wholesale" | "pending_agreement" | "not_authorized";
  agreement_notes: string;
  created_at: string;
}
